לא זהו חמץ שמוזהרים עליו – for it is not his after he has designated it (literally ‘called it by name’) and it is written in Scripture (Exodus 13:7): “no leaven shall be found [in your territory].” Yours you cannot see but you can see that of others and of “On High” (i.e., God). And this is not yours nor of your friends, for yet it has not reached the hand of the Kohen. But Rabbi Eliezer holds that since if he desires, he may bring up a case for decision [from a Sage] that is dedicated [to the Temple] in error and that is not something dedicated and it is appropriate for eating. It is found that it is his and it is the leaven of an Israelite. But Rabbi Yehoshua holds that we don’t say, ‘Since.” But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:4:1)


**שָׁלֹשׁ נָשִׁים לָשׁוֹת כְּאַחַת.** כָּל אַחַת תַּנּוּר מָלֵא, וְאֵין כָּאן חִמּוּץ, אַף עַל פִּי [שֶׁהָאַחַת] מַמְתֶּנֶת עַד שֶׁיֹּאפוּ הַשְּׁתַּיִם:


שלש נשים לשות כאחת – Each one [all together] in a full oven, and there is no fermentation, even though [that one of them] is waiting until the other two make it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:4:2)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים.** אֵין לְהָקֵל כָּל כָּךְ שֶׁיְּהוּ לָשׁוֹת כְּאַחַת, אֶלָּא שָׁלֹשׁ נָשִׁים עוֹסְקוֹת כָּל אַחַת בַּבָּצֵק שֶׁלָּהּ:


וחכ"א – one does not have to be so liberal that they would kneading all together, but three women are engaged, each one on her piece of dough.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:4:3)


**אַחַת לָשָׁה.** הָאַחֲרוֹנָה לָשָׁה כְּשֶׁחֲבֶרְתָּהּ הָאֶמְצָעִית מְקַטֶּפֶת, וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁית שֶׁלָּשָׁה תְחִלָּה אוֹפָה, נִמְצְאוּ שְׁלָשְׁתָּן עוֹסְקוֹת בְּבַת אַחַת, כָּל אַחַת בַּבָּצֵק שֶׁלָּהּ, אַחַת לָשָׁה שֶׁלָּהּ, וְאַחַת מְקַטֶּפֶת שֶׁלָּהּ, וְאַחַת אוֹפָה שֶׁלָּהּ:


אחת לשה – the concluding one kneads while her middle neighbor forms the dough and smoothens its surfaces while the third one that kneads first bakes. It is found that all three of them are engaged at the same time – each one with her own dough, one kneads hers, another forms the dough and smoothens its surfaces and one bakes hers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:4:4)


**לֹא כָל הַנָּשִׁים.** רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אַמִּלְּתֵיהּ דְּרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל קָא מְהַדַּר וְאָמַר שֶׁאֵין רָאוּי לַעֲשׂוֹת כִּדְבָרָיו, לְפִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ נָשִׁים עַצְלָנִיּוֹת וּמַחְמִיצוֹת אִם שׁוֹהוֹת כָּל כָּךְ, וְיֵשׁ תַּנּוּר שֶׁאֵינוֹ חַם מַהֵר, וְיֵשׁ עֵצִים שֶׁאֵין נִבְעָרִים מַהֵר. אֶלָּא כְּדִבְרֵי חֲכָמִים רָאוּי לַעֲשׂוֹת, לַעֲסֹק תָּמִיד בַּבָּצֵק, שֶׁכָּל זְמַן שֶׁעוֹסְקִין בַּבָּצֵק אֵינוֹ בָּא לִידֵי חִמּוּץ. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


לא כל הנשים – Rabbi Akiva [is reacting] to the words of Rabban Gamaliel when he returns and says that it is inappropriate to follow his words. Because there are lazy women which will cause fermenting with such a delay, and there is an oven that does not get hot in a hurry and there is wood that is not burned quickly, but rather, according to the words of the Sages, it is appropriate to follow – to engage at all times with the dough , for all the time that they are engaged with the dough, it does not come to fermentation. And such is the Halakha.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:4:5)


**זֶה הַכְּלָל תָּפַח.** הַבָּצֵק שֶׁבְּיָדָהּ שֶׁרוֹאָה שֶׁרוֹצֶה לִתְפֹּחַ:


זה הכלל תפח – the dough that is in her hands that she sees that it wants to swell.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:4:6)


**תִּלְטֹשׁ.** יָדָהּ בְּמַיִם צוֹנְנִין וּתְקַטְּפֶנָּה וְתִצְטַנֵּן:


תלטוש – her hand in cold water and form the dough and smoothen its surface and it will become cold.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:5:1)


**שְׂאֹר.** שֶׁלֹּא הֶחְמִיץ כָּל צָרְכּוֹ: [סָדוּק].</b> דֶּרֶךְ הַבָּצֵק כְּשֶׁמַּחְמִיץ נַעֲשֶׂה סְדָקִים סְדָקִים:


שיאור – that did not ferment all the way: [cracks] it is the manner of dough that rfermits that it makes many cracks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:5:2)


**כְּקַרְנֵי חֲגָבִים.** סֶדֶק לְכָאן וְסֶדֶק לְכָאן:


כקני חגבים – a crack to this side and a crack on the other side.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:5:3)


**זֶה וָזֶה הָאוֹכְלוֹ חַיָּב כָּרֵת.** דְּקַרְנֵי חֲגָבִים נַמִּי הוּא סָדוּק. וְאֵיזֶהוּ שְׂאֹר שֶׁפָּטוּר, זֶה שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ שׁוּם סִדּוּק, אֲבָל הִכְסִיפוּ פָנָיו כְּאָדָם שֶׁעָמְדוּ שַׂעֲרוֹתָיו מִתּוֹךְ פַּחַד [שֶׁפָּנָיו מַכְסִיפִין]. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


זה וזה האוכלו חייב כרת – for the locust’s horns also are a crack. And what is a fermentation that would be exempt? This which has no crack, but if the dough became pale and wrinkled like a person whose stair stood up out of fright [that his face grows pale]. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:6:1)


**מְבַעֲרִים אֶת הַכֹּל.** בֵּין חֻלִּין בֵּין תְּרוּמָה, חוּץ מִכְּדֵי אֲכִילָתוֹ לְשַׁבָּת:


מבערים את הכל – whether it is sacred or not sacred, except for enough for him to eat for the Savbbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:6:2)


**תְּרוּמָה מִלִּפְנֵי הַשַּׁבָּת.** שֶׁאֵין יָכוֹל לְהַאֲכִילָהּ לֹא לְזָרִים וְלֹא לִבְהֵמָה, וּלְהַשְׁהוֹתָהּ אִי אֶפְשָׁר. אֲבָל חֻלִּין אֵין צָרִיךְ לְבַעֵר אֶלָּא בִזְמַנּוֹ, שֶׁיָּכוֹל לִמְצֹא לָהֶם אוֹכְלִים הַרְבֵּה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בְּרַבִּי צָדוֹק:


תרומה מלפני השבת – because he cannot feed it neither to foreigners (i.e., non-Kohanim) nor to his cattle and to have it lay around, it is impossible. But non-sacred [leaven] he doesn’t have to remove it until its [appropriate] time for he can find for them much food. And the Halakah is according to Rabbi Elazar b’Rabbi Tzadok.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:7:1)


**הַהוֹלֵךְ.** בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר קָאֵי:


ההולך – it is referring to the fourteen [of Nisan].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:7:2)


**אִם יָכוֹל.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ שְׁהוּת בַּיּוֹם:


אם יכול – that there is time during the day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:7:3)


**לְהַצִּיל מִן הַגַּיִס.** אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל הַנִּרְדָּפִים:


להציל מן הגייס – Israelites being pursued.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:7:4)


**יְבַטֵּל בְּלִבּוֹ.** וְלֹא יַחְזֹר וַאֲפִלּוּ יֵשׁ שְׁהוּת, דְּמִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא בְּבִטּוּל בְּעָלְמָא סַגִּי:


יבטל בלבו – and he should not go back, even if there is time, since according to the Torah, mere nullification is sufficient.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:7:5)


**וְלִשְׁבֹּת שְׁבִיתַת הָרְשׁוּת.** שֶׁהָיָה הוֹלֵךְ לְהַחְשִׁיךְ עַל הַתְּחוּם וְלִקְנוֹת שְׁבִיתָה לְהַלֵּךְ מִשָּׁם וָהָלְאָה אַלְפַּיִם אַמָּה לִדְבַר הָרְשׁוּת, יַחְזֹר מִיָּד. אֲבָל לִקְנוֹת שְׁבִיתַת מִצְוָה, כְּגוֹן שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לֵילֵךְ לְמָחָר לְבֵית הָאֵבֶל אוֹ לְבֵית הַמִּשְׁתֶּה, הַיְנוּ כְּהוֹלֵךְ לִשְׁחֹט אֶת פִּסְחוֹ:


לשבות שביתה הרשות – that he was walking to wait for the nightfall on the Sabbath limit and to purchase something for the Sabbath/Holy Day to walk from there and further two thousand cubits for a matter that is for pleasure/permissible, he must return immediately [and get rid of his Hametz at home]. But if it is to purchase something for the performance of a commandment such if he needs to go tomorrow to the house of mourning or the house of rejoicing, that is like his doing to slaughter his Passover sacrifice.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:1)


**שֶׁיֶּשׁ בְּיָדוֹ בְּשַׂר קֹדֶשׁ.** שֶׁנִּפְסַל בְּיוֹצֵא. שֶׁחוֹמַת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם הִיא מְחִצָּה לְקָדָשִׁים [קַלִּים]:


שיש בידו בשר קדש – that becomes invalidated when he lives, for the wall of Jerusalem is the separator of [lighter] holy things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:2)


**אִם עָבַר צוֹפִים.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם שֶׁמִּשָּׁם רוֹאִים בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


אם עבר צופים – the name of a place that from there one sees the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:3)


**שׂוֹרְפוֹ בִמְקוֹמוֹ.** וְלֹא הִטְרִיחוּהוּ לַחֲזֹר:


שורפו במקומו – and they don’t trouble him to return [to burn the Hametz or holy meat]


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:4)


**וְאִם לָאו חוֹזֵר וְשׂוֹרְפוֹ.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ו) בַּקֹּדֶשׁ לֹא תֵאָכֵל בָּאֵשׁ תִּשָּׂרֵף, בִּמְקוֹם אֲכִילָתוֹ שְׂרֵפָתוֹ:


ולאם לאו חוזר ושורפו – As it is written (Leviticus 6:23): “ [but no purification offering may be eaten from which any blood is brought into the Tent of Meeting for expiation] in the sanctuary; any such shall be consumed in fire.” In the place where he eats it, he burns it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:5)


**זֶה וָזֶה.** חָמֵץ דְּאָמְרִינַן לְעֵיל וְנִזְכַּר שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ חָמֵץ בְּתוֹךְ בֵּיתוֹ וְכוּ', וּבְשַׂר קֹדֶשׁ שֶׁעִמּוֹ:


זה וזה – leaven as we have said above and mention that he has leaven in his house, etc., and Holy meat that is with him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:6)


**כַּבֵּיצָה.** אֲבָל בְּפָחוֹת, חָמֵץ מְבַטְּלוֹ בְלִבּוֹ, וּבְשַׂר קֹדֶשׁ שׂוֹרְפוֹ בִמְקוֹמוֹ. וְסָבַר רַבִּי מֵאִיר חֲזָרָתוֹ כְטֻמְאָתוֹ, מַה טֻּמְאַת אֳכָלִין בְּכַבֵּיצָה שֶׁאֵין אֹכֶל מִטַּמֵּא בְּפָחוֹת מִכַּבֵּיצָה, כָּךְ אֵינוֹ חוֹזֵר עַל פָּחוֹת מִכַּבֵּיצָה:


כבחתה – but less than this (i.e., an egg’s bulk), he annuls it in his heart, and Holy meat he burns in his place. And Rabbi Meir holds that his return is like his defilement. Just as defilement through eatables is like that of an egg, for food cannot defile with less than an egg’s worth, so too, he does not return on something that is less than an egg’s worth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:7)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר זֶה וָזֶה בְכַזָּיִת.** סָבַר חֲזָרָתוֹ כְאִסּוּרוֹ, מָה אִסּוּרוֹ בְּכַזַּיִת, שֶׁעַל כַּזַּיִת חָמֵץ אוֹ בְּשַׂר קֹדֶשׁ חַיָּב, כָּךְ עַל כַּזַּיִת חוֹזֵר:


רבי יהודה אומר זה וזה בכזית – He holds that his return is like its prohibition. Just as its prohibition is an olive’s bulk-worth, for an a olive’s worth of leaven or of Holy meat, he is liable, so too, for a olive’s bulk, he returns.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 3:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/3:8:8)


**בְּשַׂר קֹדֶשׁ.** מִשּׁוּם חֻמְרָא דְקָדָשִׁים חוֹזֵר אַף עַל כַּזַּיִת, חָמֵץ דְּחֻלִּין אֵינוֹ חוֹזֵר אֶלָּא עַל כַּבֵּיצָה. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


בשר קודש – because of the stringency of Holy meat, he returns even for an olive bulk’s worth. Leaven of non-sacred produce he does not go back other than on an egg’s bulk. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:1:1)


**מְקוֹם שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ עַד חֲצוֹת.** עַד חֲצִי הַיּוֹם:


מקום שנהגו עד חצות – until half the day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:1:2)


**שֶׁלֹּא לַעֲשׂוֹת.** כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא טָרוּד בִּמְלָאכָה וְיִשְׁכַּח בִּעוּר חָמֵץ וּשְׁחִיטַת פֶּסַח וְתִקּוּן מַצָּה שֶׁל מִצְוָה:


שלא לעשות – in order that he not be busily engaged by his work and he would forget the [obligation of] ridding of the leavened products and the sacrifice of the Passover offering and the preparation of the Matzah for the Mitzvah [of the Seder].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:1:3)


**וְאַל יְשַׁנֶּה אָדָם.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, הַהוֹלֵךְ מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁאֵין עוֹשִׂין לְמָקוֹם שֶׁעוֹשִׂין נוֹתְנִין עָלָיו חֻמְרֵי מָקוֹם שֶׁיָּצָא מִשָּׁם וְלֹא יַעֲשֶׂה, דְּהָא דְּקָיְמָא לָן אַל יְשַׁנֶּה אָדָם מִמִּנְהַג הָעִיר אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי הַמַּחֲלֹקֶת, וְכָאן אֵין מַחֲלֹקֶת, דְּמַאי קָא אָמַרְתְּ הָרוֹאֶה אוֹתוֹ בָטֵל אוֹמֵר זֶה שֶׁבָּטֵל חוֹשֵׁב שֶׁהַמְּלָאכָה אֲסוּרָה וְחוֹלֵק עַל כֻּלָּנוּ, לֹא אָמַר הָכִי, אֶלָּא אָמַר אֵין לוֹ מְלָאכָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי כַּמָּה בַטְלָנִים אִיכָּא בְשׁוּקָא כָּל יְמוֹת הַשָּׁנָה. וְהָא דִתְנַן נוֹתְנִים עָלָיו חֻמְרֵי מָקוֹם שֶׁיָּצָא מִשָּׁם, אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא בִּזְמַן שֶׁדַּעְתּוֹ לַחְזֹר לִמְקוֹמוֹ, אֲבָל אֵין דַּעְתּוֹ לַחְזֹר לִמְקוֹמוֹ נוֹהֵג כְּמִנְהַג אַנְשֵׁי מָקוֹם שֶׁהָלַךְ לְשָׁם, בֵּין לְקֻלָּא בֵּין לְחֻמְרָא:


אל שינה אדם – this is how it should be understood: A person who walks from a place where they don’t do [work] to a place where the do [work], they should place upon him the stringencies of the place where he left from there and not do it (i.e., work), and that is what we hold: A person should not vary from the local custom of the city, it is not because of disputes for here there is no dispute. For what that you said: A person sees him idle would say that this one who is idle is because work is forbidden and that he disputes against all of us; he did not say this, but rather he said: he has no work for how many idle individuals are there in the marketplace all the days of the year. And that which we teach [in this Mishnah] that we lay upon him the strict rules followed in the place from which he has gone forth, is not [in effect] other than when it at the time when it is his intention to return to his place; but if he does not have the intention to return to his place, he practices according to the custom of the people of the place where he went to , whether for leniencies or for stringencies.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:2:1)


**הַמּוֹלִיךְ פֵּרוֹת שְׁבִיעִית מִמְּקוֹם שֶׁכָּלוּ.** לַחַיָּה מִן הַשָּׂדֶה וְחַיָּבִין עָלָיו בְּנֵי מְקוֹמוֹ לְבַעֲרָן מִן הַבַּיִת. וְהוֹלִיכָן זֶה לְמָקוֹם שֶׁלֹּא כָלוּ, וּבְנֵי אוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם עֲדַיִן אוֹכְלִין הֵן מִן הַמְכֻנָּסִים לַבַּיִת, חַיָּב לְבַעֵר מִשּׁוּם חֻמְרֵי הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁיָּצָא מִשָּׁם:


המוליך פירות שביעית ממקום שכלו – to a beast from the field and the people of his place are liable to remove it from the house. And they bring this to a place where they had not yet come to an end, and the people of that place are still eating them from that which were brought into the house, and they are liable to remove them because of the stringencies of the place from which they left.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:2:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמַר אוֹמְרִים לוֹ צֵא וְהָבֵא לְךָ אַף אָתָּה.** פְּלֻגְתָא דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה וְתַנָּא קַמָּא בְּכוֹבֵשׁ שְׁלֹשָׁה מִינֵי יָרָק בְּחֹמֶץ אוֹ בְצִיר לְהִתְקַיֵּם, וּשְׁנַיִם מֵהֶם כָּלוּ לַחַיָּה מִן הַשָּׂדֶה וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁי לֹא כָלָה, תַּנָּא קַמָּא סָבַר אוֹכֵל מֵאוֹתָן שֶׁכָּלוּ עַל סְמַךְ אוֹתוֹ שֶׁלֹּא כָלָה, הוֹאִיל וְהֵן בְּחָבִית אַחַת. וְהָכִי מִשְׁתְּמַע לְמִלְּתֵיהּ, מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁלֹּא כָלוּ לְמָקוֹם שֶׁכָּלוּ לְגַמְרֵי חַיָּבִין לְבַעֵר, אֲבָל לֹא כָלוּ כֻלָּן אֶלָּא מִקְצָתָן אוֹכֵל אַף מִמִּין הַכָּלֶה כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא כָלוּ כֻלָּן, דְּאוֹכְלִין עַל סְמַךְ הָאַחֲרוֹן. וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אוֹמְרִים לוֹ צֵא וְהָבֵא לְךָ אַף אַתָּה מֵאוֹתוֹ הַמִּין שֶׁכָּלָה כְּמוֹ שֶׁהֵבִיא זֶה הָאִישׁ וְלֹא תִמְצָא, הִלְכָּךְ אֵינוֹ אוֹכֵל אֶלָּא מֵהַמִּין שֶׁלֹּא כָלָה, דְּאֵין אוֹכְלִין מֵהַמִּינִין שֶׁכָּלוּ עַל סְמַךְ הַמִּין שֶׁלֹּא כָּלָה, וְחַיָּב לְבַעֵר הַמִּינִין שֶׁכָּלוּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה, דְּקָאֵי בְשִׁיטְתֵיהּ דְּרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל שֶׁהֲלָכָה כְמוֹתוֹ בְּמַסֶּכֶת שְׁבִיעִית פֶּרֶק הַפֵּיגָם:


רבי יהודה אומר אומרים לו צא והבא לך אף אתה – There is a dispute between Rabbi Yehuda and the First Teacher [of the Mishnah] (Pesahim 52a) where one pickles/preserves three kinds of greens in vinegar or in brine to preserve and two of them came to an end for the beast from the field and the third had not yet come to an end. The First Tanna holds that one eats from those which had come to an end with the support of that which had not yet come to an end, since they are in one barrel. And this is astonishing for this thing, from a place where they did not come to an end to a place where they completely came to an end, one is obligated to remove them. But, if all of them did not come to an end, but part of them, one can eat even from the species that had come to an end all the while that all of them had not coe to an end. And one eats on support of the final ones. But Rabbi Yehuda states: We say to him: Go and bring some for yourself – even you, from that species that was completed, like this person brought and it would not be found. Therefore, one does not eat other than from the species that has not yet completed, for we don’t eat from those species that were completed through the support of the species that was not yet completed. And one is obligated to remove those species that were completed. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda that refers to the approach of Rabban Gamaliel where the Halakha is according to him in the Tractate Sheviit (chapter 9), the “Chapter of Rue.”



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:3:1)


**מְקוֹם שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ שֶׁלֹּא לִמְכֹּר.** דְּהֶחְמִירוּ עַל עַצְמָן דִּלְמָא אָתֵי לִמְכֹּר גַּסָּה:


מקום שנהגו שלא למכור – since they were stringent upon themselves perhaps they would come to see a large [animal].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:3:2)


**וּבְכָל מָקוֹם אֵין מוֹכְרִין לָהֶם בְּהֵמָה גַסָּה.** דְּגָזוּר רַבָּנָן שֶׁמָּא יַשְׁאִיל אוֹ יַשְׂכִּיר בְּהֶמְתּוֹ לְעוֹבֵד כּוֹכָבִים, וְאָדָם מְצֻוֶּה עַל שְׁבִיתַת בְּהֶמְתּוֹ. אִי נַמִּי, פְּעָמִים שֶׁמּוֹכְרָהּ לוֹ עֶרֶב שַׁבָּת עִם חֲשֵׁכָה, וְצוֹעֵק לָהּ הַיִּשְׂרָאֵל כְּדֵי שֶׁתֵּלֵךְ לִפְנֵי הַקּוֹנֶה, וְהִיא מַכֶּרֶת קוֹלוֹ וְהוֹלֶכֶת מֵחֲמָתוֹ, וְנִמְצָא מְחַמֵּר אַחַר בְּהֶמְתּוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת:


ובכל מקום אין מוכרים להם בהמה גסה – since the Rabbis made a decree lest a person lend out or rent his animal to idolaters, and a person is commanded on resting of his animal. Alternatively, sometimes that he sells it on the eve of the Sabbath (i.e., Friday) at dusk and the Jew cries to it (i.e., the animal) in order that it should go before the purchaser and she reis animal on Shabbat recognizes his (i.e., the former owner’s) voice and she goes from his anger, and it is found that he directs a laden beast’s motions by walking behind it on Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:3:3)


**עֲגָלִים וּסְיָחִין.** קְטַנִּים, אַף עַל גַּב דְּלָאו בְּנֵי מְלָאכָה נִינְהוּ, מִחַלְּפֵי בִּמְכִירַת גְּדוֹלִים. וּשְׁבוּרִים מִחַלְּפֵי בִּמְכִירַת שְׁלֵמִים:


עגלים וסייחין – small, even though they are not doing work, they are exchanged through the sale of larger animals and broken animals are exchanged with the sale of whole animals.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:3:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַתִּיר בִּשְׁבוּרָה.** דְּאֵינָהּ בַּת מְלָאכָה לְעוֹלָם. אֲבָל בַּעֲגָלִים וּסְיָחִים מוֹדֶה, כֵּיוָן דִּלְכִי גָּדְלֵי הָווּ בְנֵי מְלָאכָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה מתיר בשבורה – that is never capable of working ever. But calves and foals, he admits, that since when they grow, they are capable of working. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:3:5)


**בֶּן בְּתֵירָא מַתִּיר בְּסוּס.** הוֹאִיל וְלִרְכִיבָה עוֹמֵד, וְהַחַי נוֹשֵׂא אֶת עַצְמוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּבֶן בְּתֵירָא. וְעַל יְדֵי סַרְסוּר שָׁרֵי לִמְכֹּר בְּהֵמָה לְעוֹבֵד כּוֹכָבִים בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵין הַבְּעָלִים מְצוּיִין שָׁם בְּעֵת הַמְּכִירָה, דְּלֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ שֶׁמָּא יַשְׁאִילֶנָּה וְיַשְׂכִּירֶנָּה, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵינָהּ שֶׁלּוֹ. וְשֶׁמָּא תֵלֵךְ מֵחֲמַת קוֹלוֹ וְנִמְצָא מְחַמֵּר לְהָא נַמִּי לֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ, שֶׁאֵינָהּ מַכֶּרֶת אֶת קוֹלוֹ:


בן בתירא מתיר בסוס – since it stands for riding and the living carries itself, but the Halakha is not according to Ben Beteyra. But via middleman, it is permitted to sell it to an idolater when the owners are not found there at the time of the sale, for one should not suspect lest he lend it out or rent it for it is not his. And lest she should go on the strength of his voice and he is found that he directs a laden beast’s motions for that also, one should not suspect, for she doesn’t recognize his voice.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:4:1)


**מְקוֹם שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ שֶׁלֹּא לֶאֱכֹל.** שֶׁנִּרְאֶה כְאוֹכֵל פְּסָחִים בְּחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ:


מקום שנהגו שלא לאכול – since it appears like one is eating Passover sacrifices outside the land [of Israel].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:4:2)


**שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ לְהַדְלִיק אֶת הַנֵּר בְּיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁיּוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים אָסוּר בְּתַשְׁמִישׁ הַמִּטָּה, וְכָל זְמַן שֶׁהַנֵּר דּוֹלֵק לֹא יְשַׁמֵּשׁ שֶׁאָסוּר לָאָדָם לְשַׁמֵּשׁ מִטָּתוֹ לְאוֹר הַנֵּר:


שנהגו להדליק את הנר ביום הכפורים – because on Yom Kippur it is prohibited to engage in sexual relations, and all the time that the candle is kindled, they would not engage [in sexual relations] as it is prohibited for a person to engage in sexual relations by the light of a candle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:4:3)


**שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ שֶׁלֹּא לְהַדְלִיק.** שֶׁמָּא יִרְאֶה אִשְׁתּוֹ וְתִשָּׂא חֵן בְּעֵינָיו וִישַׁמֵּשׁ:


שנהגו שלא להדליק – lest he see his wife and she finds favor in his eyes and he engages in sexual relations.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:4:4)


**וּמַדְלִיקִין בְּבָתֵּי כְנֵסִיּוֹת.** וּבְכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין אִישׁ וְאִשְׁתּוֹ מִתְיַחֲדִים שָׁם:


ומדליקין בבתי כנסיות – and in every place where men and his wife are not together [alone] there.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:5:1)


**תַּלְמִידֵי חֲכָמִים בְּטֵלִים.** מִמְּלַאכְתָּן כָּל אוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַסִּיחוּ דַעְתָּם מֵאֲבֵלוּת:


תלמידי חכמים בטלים – [Sages are idle] from their work all that day, in order that their attentions would not be diverted from mourning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:5:2)


**לְעוֹלָם יַעֲשֶׂה אָדָם עַצְמוֹ כְּתַלְמִיד חָכָם.** וְלֹא מֵחֲזֵי כְיֻהֲרָא, שֶׁהָרוֹאֶה אוֹתוֹ בָטֵל אוֹמֵר אֵין לוֹ מְלָאכָה לַעֲשׂוֹת וְלֹא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנּוֹהֵג בּוֹ אִסּוּר:


לעולם יעשה אדם עצמו כתלמיד חכם – and it does not appear like haughtiness, for a person who sees him idle would say that he has no work to do and not because he practices a prohibition upon himself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:5:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים בִּיהוּדָה וְכוּ'.** חֲכָמִים סְבִירָא לְהוּ דַּעֲשִׂיַּת מְלָאכָה בְּעַרְבֵי פְסָחִים לָאו בְּמִנְהָגָא תַּלְיָא מִלְּתָא, אֶלָּא בִּיהוּדָה הָיוּ מַתִּירִים וּבַגָּלִיל אוֹסְרִים אִסּוּר גָּמוּר, וְלֹא מִכֹּחַ מִנְהָג:


וחכמים אומרים ביהודה – The Sages hold that doing work on the eve of Passover is not something dependent upon a custom but rather in Judea, they would permit it and in the Galilee, it would be a complete prohibition, and not from the strength of the custom.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:5:4)


**הַלַּיְלָה.** לֵיל אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר, לְאַנְשֵׁי גָלִיל שֶׁאוֹסְרִים לַעֲשׂוֹת מְלָאכָה בְּעַרְבֵי פְסָחִים:


הלילה – the night of the fourteenth [of Nisan]; for the people of the Galiele who prohibit doing work on the eves of Passover.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:5:5)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹסְרִים.** לָהֶם כְּכָל שְׁאָר יָמִים טוֹבִים שֶׁאֲסוּרִים בַּעֲשִׂיַּת מְלָאכָה, שֶׁהַלַּיְלָה הוֹלֵךְ אַחַר הַיּוֹם:


ב"ש אוסרים – [prohibits] them like the other Holy days where it is prohibited to do work for the night follows after the day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:5:6)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל מַתִּירִים.** מִידֵי דַּהֲוָה אַתַּעֲנִית, שֶׁהַיּוֹם אָסוּר בַּאֲכִילָה וְהַלַּיְלָה מֻתָּר:


וב"ה מתירים – something that resembles a fast [day] where the day is prohiibred in eating and in the nighttime it is permissible.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:6:1)


**כָּל מְלָאכָה.** שֶׁהִיא לְצֹרֶךְ הַמּוֹעֵד וְהִתְחִיל בָּהּ קֹדֶם אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר, גּוֹמְרָהּ בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר, וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ שֶׁלֹּא לַעֲשׂוֹת מְלָאכָה. אֲבָל מְלָאכָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ לְצֹרֶךְ הַמּוֹעֵד, בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת מְלָאכָה עוֹשִׂין, וּמָקוֹם שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ שֶׁלֹּא לַעֲשׂוֹת, אֲפִלּוּ הִתְחִיל בָּהּ קֹדֶם אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר אֵינוֹ גּוֹמְרָהּ:


כל מלאכה – [all work] which is for the needs of he Festival/Holiday and he began it prior to the fourteenth [of Nisan] may complete it on the fourteenth. And even in a place where they had the practice of not doing work. But work that is not for needs of he Festival, in a place where it was the practice to do work, they may do it, but I n a place where it was the practice not to do it, even if he began it prior to the fourteenth, he may not complete it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:6:2)


**חַיָּטִים.** שָׁרֵי בְאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר בְּכָל מָקוֹם, שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ בָהֶן בְּחֻלּוֹ שֶׁל מוֹעֵד קֻלָּא יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁאָר אֻמָּנֻיּוֹת, שֶׁהֶדְיוֹט שֶׁאֵינוֹ אֻמָּן בְּכָךְ תּוֹפֵר כְּדַרְכּוֹ, הִלְכָּךְ בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר דְּקִיל מֵחֹל הַמּוֹעֵד אֲפִלּוּ אֻמָּן נַמִּי שָׁרֵי:


חייטים – are permitted on the fourteenth [of Nisan] in every place, for we found regarding them a leniency even greater than other artisans on the Intermediate Days of the Festival for the commoner who is not an artisan in this sews/mends in the normal manner. Therefore, on the fourteenth [of Nisan] which is more lenient than the Intermediate Days of the Festival, even an artisan is also permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:6:3)


**וְהַסַּפָּרִים וְהַכּוֹבְסִין.** שֶׁכֵּן הַבָּא מִמְּדִינַת הַיָּם וְהַיּוֹצֵא מִבֵּית הָאֲסוּרִים מְסַפְּרִים וּמְכַבְּסִים בְּחֹל הַמּוֹעֵד, וְכֵיוָן דְּאַשְׁכְּחָן בָּהֶן צַד הֶתֵּר בְּחֹל הַמּוֹעֵד, בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר דְּקִיל שָׁרֵי לְכֻלֵּי עָלְמָא:


והספרים והכובסים – and similarly one who comes from abroad and one who leaves from prison takes haircuts and does laundry on the Intermediate Days of the Festival, and since we find for them an indirect legal permission on the Intermediate Days of the Festival, on the fourteenth [of Nisan] which is lenient, we permit for the entire world.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:6:4)


**אַף הָרַצְעָנִים.** שֶׁכֵּן עוֹלֵי רְגָלִים מְתַקְּנִין מִנְעֲלֵיהֶן בְּחֹל הַמּוֹעֵד. וַחֲכָמִים סָבְרֵי אֵין לְמֵדִין תְּחִלַּת מְלֶאכֶת רַצְעָנִים שֶׁעוֹשִׂין מִנְעָלִים חֲדָשִׁים תְּחִלָּה, מִסּוֹף מְלָאכָה תִּקּוּן מִנְעָלִים שֶׁל עוֹלֵי רְגָלִים. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


אף הרצענים – for similarly those who come up [to Jerusalem] for the Festival repair their shoes on the Intermediate Days of the Festival. And the Sages hold that we don’t learn beginning the work of shoemakers who make new shoes first from the end of work the repair of shoes of those who go up [to Jerusalem] for the Festival. And the Halakha is according to the Sages (see Pesahim 55b).



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:7:1)


**מוֹשִׁיבִין שׁוֹבָכִין.** שׁוֹבְכֵי יוֹנִים שֶׁיִּהְיוּ יוֹשְׁבִים לְגַדֵּל אֶפְרוֹחִים, מוֹשִׁיבִין אוֹתָם לְכַתְּחִלָּה בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר:


מושיבין שובכין – (Possible misinterpretation by Bartenura here – as the Mishnah is clearly speaking of chicken-coops). Dove cotes on which they would sit to raise the young birds, we place them there ab initio on the fourteenth [of Nisan].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:7:2)


**וְתַרְנְגֹלֶת שֶׁבָּרְחָה.** בְּחֻלּוֹ שֶׁל מוֹעֵד. דְּאִלּוּ בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר הַשְׁתָּא אוֹתוֹבֵי שׁוֹבָכִין לְכַתְּחִלָּה אָמַרְתְּ דְּשָׁרֵי, אַהְדוֹרֵי מִבַּעְיָא. אֶלָּא הַתַּרְנְגֹלֶת שֶׁבָּרְחָה בְּחֻלּוֹ שֶׁל מוֹעֵד קָאָמַר דְּמַחֲזִירִין, וְהוּא שֶׁיָּשְׁבָה עַל הַבֵּיצִים שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים קֹדֶם שֶׁבָּרְחָה, דְּשׁוּב אֵין הַבֵּיצִים רְאוּיִים לַאֲכִילָה וְדָבָר הָאָבֵד הוּא, הִלְכָּךְ אִם מֵתָה מוֹשִׁיב אַחֶרֶת תַּחְתֶּיהָ מִשּׁוּם הֶפְסֵד דְּבֵיצִים:


ותרנגולת שברחה – on the Intermediate Days of the Festival [of Passover] whereas on the fourteenth, now that they have restore the coops, you might say that they are permissible ab initio, it is needed to return them, but rather, he said that the chickens who fled on the Intermediate Days of the Festival, we return them. For this is the case where she (i.e., the hen) has sat on the eggs for three days prior to fleeing , for the eggs are not appropriate for eating and it is a business which cannot be postponed without irretrievable loss (see Moed Katan 11a). Therefore, if it died, we sit another in its place because of the [potential] loss of the eggs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:7:3)


**גּוֹרְפִין.** מַשְׁלִיכִין לַחוּץ:


גורפין – throw/cast outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:7:4)


**וּבַמּוֹעֵד.** שֶׁחָמוּר יוֹתֵר, אֵין מַשְׁלִיכִין לַחוּץ אֶלָּא מְסַלְּקִין לַצְּדָדִין:


ובמועד – which is more stringent, we don’t cast them outside, but rather remove them to the sides.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:7:5)


**מוֹלִיכִין וּמְבִיאִין כֵּלִים מִבֵּית הָאֻמָּן.** בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ לְצֹרֶךְ הַמּוֹעֵד:


ומביאין כלים מבית האומן – on the fourteenth [of Nisan] and even though it is not for the needs of the Festival.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:1)


**עָשׂוּ אַנְשֵׁי יְרִיחוֹ.** הָיוּ נוֹהֲגִין לַעֲשׂוֹת אַנְשֵׁי יְרִיחוֹ:


עשו אנשי יריחו – it was the practice of the people of Jericho to do.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:2)


**מַרְכִּיבִין דְּקָלִים.** עָנָף רַךְ שֶׁל דֶּקֶל זָכָר מַרְכִּיבוֹ בְדֶקֶל נְקֵבָה, שֶׁהַזָּכָר עוֹשֶׂה פֵרוֹת וְהַנְּקֵבָה אֵינָהּ עוֹשָׂה פֵרוֹת:


מרכיבין דקלים – a soft branch of a male palm tree they graft it with a female palm tree, since the male makes fruit and the female does not make fruit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:3)


**כָּל הַיּוֹם.** כְּלוֹמַר בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר:


כל היום – that is to say, on the fourteenth [of Nisan].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:4)


**וְכוֹרְכִין אֶת שְׁמַע.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ אוֹמְרִים בָּרוּךְ שֵׁם כְּבוֹד מַלְכוּתוֹ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד בִּקְרִיאַת שְׁמַע. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ מַפְסִיקִין בֵּין אֶחָד לִוְאָהַבְתָּ, שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לְהַאֲרִיךְ בְּאֶחָד וּלְהַפְסִיק בֵּין מַלְכוּת שָׁמַיִם לִדְבָרִים אֲחֵרִים:


וכורכין את שמע – that they would not say: “Praised be the Name of God’s glorious Kingdom forever and ever,” during the recitation of the Shema. Another interpretation. They would not stop between [the word God is] “one” and “you shall love” (Deuteronomy 6:4 and 6:5), for one must lengthen [the word] “one” and to interrupt/stop between the Kingship of Heaven and other things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:5)


**וְקוֹצְרִין וְגוֹדְשִׁין.** עוֹשִׂין גָּדִישׁ מִן הֶחָדָשׁ לִפְנֵי הָעֹמֶר, וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא אָתֵי לְמֵיכַל מִנֵּיהּ:


וקוצרין וגודשין – they make a heap [of a stack of grain] from the new grain before the Omer, and we are not troubled that perhaps they might come to eat from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:6)


**וְלֹא מִחוּ בְיָדָם.** כָּל הַשִּׁשָּׁה דְבָרִים שֶׁעָשׂוּ, כֻּלָּם שֶׁלֹּא בִרְצוֹן חֲכָמִים הָיוּ עוֹשִׂין, אֶלָּא שֶׁעַל אֵלּוּ הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה לֹא מִחוּ וְעַל שְׁלֹשָׁה הָאַחֲרוֹנִים מִחוּ:


ולא מיחו בידם – all these six things that they did, all of them were not done with the Sages’ willingness, but on these they did not reprove them, and on the latter three they reproved them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:7)


**מַתִּירִין לֶאֱכֹל גִּמְזִיּוֹת שֶׁל הֶקְדֵּשׁ.** גִּדּוּלִים שֶׁגָּדְלוּ בְּאִילָן שֶׁל הֶקְדֵּשׁ, דְּקָסָבְרֵי אֵין אִסּוּר אֶלָּא הַפְּרִי עַצְמוֹ שֶׁהֻקְדַּשׁ. וְאִית סְפָרִים דְּגָרְסֵי מַתְּזִיר, כְּלוֹמַר חוֹתְכִים וְכוֹרְתִים עַנְפֵי אִילָנוֹת שֶׁל הֶקְדֵּשׁ שֶׁצָּמְחוּ אַחַר שֶׁהִקְדִּישׁוּ הָאִילָן, לֵהָנוֹת מֵהֶן:


מתירין לאכול גמזיות של הקדש – growth that grew on trees dedicated to the Temple treasury, as they hold that there is no prohibition other than on the fruit itself that had been dedicated to the Temple treasury, and there are books that have the reading, MATZIR (see Melekhet Shlomo, who states that this reading is in error – and should be M’TIZIN/to chop off, cause to fly off), that is to say, they dissect and cut off branches of the trees belonging to the Temple treasury that grew after they had dedicated the tree to the Temple treasury, to benefit from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:8)


**וְאוֹכְלִין.** בְּשַׁבָּת וְיוֹם טוֹב פֵּרוֹת שֶׁנִּמְצְאוּ תַּחַת הָאִילָן, וְלֹא נוֹדַע אִם נָשְׁרוּ מֵאֶמֶשׁ וְהֵם מֻתָּרִים, אוֹ נָשְׁרוּ הַיּוֹם וַאֲסוּרִים:


ואוכלין – on Shabbat and Holy Days/Yom Tov [they eat] fruit that were found under the tree and it was not known if they fell from last night and they are permitted, or they fell today and are prohibited.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:9)


**וְנוֹתְנִים פֵּאָה לַיָּרָק.** וַאֲנַן קָיְמָא לָן דְּכָל דָּבָר שֶׁאֵין אָדָם מַכְנִיסוֹ לְקִיּוּם אֵינוֹ חַיָּב בְּפֵאָה, וְיָרָק דָּבָר שֶׁאֵין מַכְנִיסוֹ לְקִיּוּם הוּא:


ונותנים פאה לירק – and we hold for ourselves that every thing where a person does not bring them in for storage (not for immediate use) is not obligated [for the commandment] of [leaving] the corner. And vegetables are a thing that one does not bring them in for storage (not for immediate use).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:8:10)


**וּמִחוּ בְיָדָם חֲכָמִים.** מִשּׁוּם דְּקָא מַפְקְעֵי לַהּ מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר, וְהָעֲנִיִּים אוֹכְלִים אוֹתָהּ בְּטִבְלָם, כִּסְבוּרִין שֶׁהוּא פֵאָה, וּפֵאָה פְטוּרָה מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר מִשּׁוּם דְּהֶפְקֵר הִיא:


ומיחו בידם חכמים – because they exclude it from the tithes, and the poor eat it while they are eatables forbidden pending the separation of sacred gifts, thinking that it is “Peah”/corner of the field and the corner of the field is exempt from tithes because it is ownerless.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:9:1)


**גֵּרַר עַצְמוֹת אָבִיו.** מִשּׁוּם כַּפָּרָה. וְלֹא קְבָרוֹ לְפִי כְבוֹדוֹ עַל מִטָּה נָאָה, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְגַּנֶּה עַל רִשְׁעוֹ וְיִוָּסְרוּ הָרְשָׁעִים:


גירר עצמות אביו – because of atonement. And he did not bury him according to his honor on a nice bier in order that he would be put to shame on his wickedness and that the wicked would be tormented.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:9:2)


**כִּתֵּת נְחַשׁ הַנְּחֹשֶׁת.** כְּדִכְתִיב בְּדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים, לְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ טוֹעִין אַחֲרָיו:


כתת נחש הנחשת – as it is written in Chronicles (2 Kings 18:4 – “He broke into pieces the bronze serpent that Moses had made, for until that time the Israelites had been offering sacrifices to it; it was called Nehushtan”) because they would err after him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:9:3)


**וְגָנַז סֵפֶר רְפוּאוֹת.** לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה לִבָּם נִכְנָע עַל חֳלְיָם אֶלָּא מִתְרַפְּאִים מִיָּד. וְהָרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ, סֵפֶר רְפוּאוֹת, סֵפֶר מוֹדִיעַ מֵעִנְיְנֵי צוּרוֹת הַכּוֹכָבִים וְהַטֶּלֶסְמָאוֹת, שֶׁצּוּרָה פְלוֹנִית הָעֲשׂוּיָה בִּזְמַן וְעֵת יָדוּעַ מְרַפֵּאת מֵחֹלִי פְלוֹנִי, וְזֶה הָיָה קָרוֹב לְהַטְעוֹת הַבְּרִיּוֹת אַחַר עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים וּלְכָךְ גְּנָזוֹ:


וגנז ספר רפואות – because their hearts were not humbled by their illnesses but they were healed immediately. And Maimonides explained, that the book of healing, was a book informing of the matters of the forms of the stars and their witchcraft, that a certain form was made at a known time and period while curing from specific illness. And this was close to causing humans to error after idolatry and for that reason, the books were hidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 4:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/4:9:4)


**עִבֵּר נִיסָן בְּנִיסָן.** לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּכְנַס נִיסָן נִמְלַךְ וְעָשָׂה אֲדָר שֵׁנִי. וְהַכָּתוּב אוֹמֵר (שמות יב) הַחֹדֶשׁ הַזֶּה לָכֶם, זֶה נִיסָן, וְאֵין אַחֵר נִיסָן. וְלֹא עִבֵּר חִזְקִיָּה לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּכְנַס נִיסָן מַמָּשׁ, אֶלָּא יוֹם שְׁלֹשִׁים שֶׁל אֲדָר עִבֵּר אֶת הַשָּׁנָה, וְאֲנַן קָיְמָא לָן אֵין מְעַבְּרִין אֶת הַשָּׁנָה בִּשְׁלֹשִׁים שֶׁל אֲדָר, הוֹאִיל וְרָאוּי לְקָבְעוֹ נִיסָן:


עיבר ניסן בניסן – after Nisan had begun, he took counsel and it was made Second Adar, and the Biblical verse states (Exodus 12:2): “This month shall be for you the first of months.” This is Nisan and there is nothing after Nisan, and Hezekiah did not intercalate the year after Nisan had already begun, but on the thirtieth day of Adar, he intercalated the year. And we hold that we do not intercalate the year on the thirtieth of Adar since it is appropriate to establish it as Nisan.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:1:1)


**תָּמִיד נִשְׁחָט.** תָּמִיד שֶׁל בֵּין הָעַרְבַּיִם נִשְׁחָט כָּל שְׁאָר יְמוֹת הַשָּׁנָה בִּשְׁמֹנֶה וּמֶחֱצָה. דִּזְמַן שְׁחִיטַת הַתָּמִיד מִכִּי יִנָּטוּ צִלְלֵי עֶרֶב שֶׁהוּא מִשֵּׁשׁ שָׁעוֹת וּמֶחֱצָה וּלְמַעְלָה שֶׁחַמָּה נוֹטָה לַמַּעֲרָב, שֶׁחֲצִי שֵׁשׁ וַחֲצִי שֶׁבַע הִיא עוֹמֶדֶת בְּאֶמְצַע הָרָקִיעַ וְאֵין צֵל נוֹטֶה אֶלָּא צֵל כָּל אָדָם תַּחְתָּיו. וּמְאַחֲרִין שְׁחִיטָתוֹ שְׁתֵּי שָׁעוֹת אַחַר זְמַנּוֹ מִפְּנֵי נְדָרִים וּנְדָבוֹת, שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְהַקְרִיבָם אַחַר תָּמִיד שֶׁל בֵּין הָעַרְבַּיִם, כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא ו) וְהִקְטִיר עָלֶיהָ חֶלְבֵי הַשְּׁלָמִים, עַל עוֹלַת הַבֹּקֶר הַשְׁלֵם כָּל הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת כֻּלָּן, שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא דָּבָר מְאֻחָר לְתָמִיד שֶׁל בֵּין הָעַרְבַּיִם. וַאֲפִלּוּ בְשַׁבָּת שֶׁאֵין קְרֵבִים נְדָרִים וּנְדָבוֹת, מְאַחֲרִים זְמַן שְׁחִיטַת תָּמִיד שֶׁל בֵּין הָעַרְבַּיִם מִשּׁוּם סְרַךְ נְדָרִים וּנְדָבוֹת דְּחֹל. וּבְעֶרֶב פֶּסַח שֶׁהַפֶּסַח קָרֵב אַחַר תָּמִיד שֶׁל בֵּין הָעַרְבַּיִם, מַקְדְּמִינַן לֵיהּ שָׁעָה אַחַת וְעָבְדִינַן לֵיהּ בְּשֶׁבַע וּמֶחֱצָה:


תמיד נשחט – the daily whole offering made at the time between the beginning of the decline of the sun and sunset (i.e., afternoon) is slaughtered all the rest of the days of the year at the eighth and one-half hour [of the day] (=2:30 pm). For the time of the slaughter of the daily whole offering is from when the shadows of the evening decline which is from six and one-half hours (=12:30 pm) as the sun declines to the west, as for half of the sixth and half of the seventh [hours], it (i.e., the sun) stands in the middle of the firmament. And the shadow does not decline other than the shadow of each person underneath it. And we make the slaughter [of the sacrifice] later by two hours after its time because of the vows and donations for it is impossible to offer them after the daily whole offering of the afternoon, as it is written (Leviticus 6:5): “and turn into smoke the fat parts of the offerings of well-being.” On the daily whole offering of the morning, he completed all the sacrifices entirely, so that there should not be anything later than the daily whole offering of the afternoon. And even on Shabbat where we do not offer vows and donations, we make the time of the slaughtering of the daily offering later because of the habit of vows and donations of the weekday. And on the Eve of Passover, when the Passover offering is made after the daily whole offering of the afternoon is sacrificed, we advance it one hour and do it at seven and one-half hours (=1:30 pm).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:1:2)


**חָל לִהְיוֹת בְּעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת.** דְּאִיכָּא נַמִּי צְלִיַּת הַפֶּסַח דְּלֹא דָחְיָא שַׁבָּת וְצָרִיךְ לִצְלוֹתוֹ מִבְּעוֹד יוֹם, מַקְדְּמִינַן לֵיהּ וּמוֹתְבִינַן לֵיהּ אַדִּינֵיהּ, שֶׁיִּהְיֶה נִשְׁחָט בְּשֵׁשׁ וּמֶחֱצָה:


חל להיות בע"ש – for there is also the roasting of the Passover sacrifice that does not override the Sabbath, and one must roast it while it is still daylight, we advance it establish it according to the law that it would be slaughtered at six and one-half hours (=12:30 pm).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:1:3)


**וְקָרֵב בְּשֶׁבַע וּמֶחֱצָה.** גְּמַר הַקְרָבָתוֹ בְּשֶׁבַע וּמֶחֱצָה, שֶׁשּׁוֹהִין שָׁעָה אַחַת בַּעֲשִׂיָּתוֹ:


וקרב בשבע ומחצה – the completion of its offering is at seven and one-half hours (1:30 pm) for we tarry one hour while performing this ritual.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:2:1)


**שֶׁשְּׁחָטוֹ שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמוֹ.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁשְּׁחָטוֹ לְשֵׁם שְׁלָמִים, אוֹ קִבֵּל הַדָּם בַּמִּזְרָק, אוֹ הוֹלִיךְ הַדָּם לַמִּזְבֵּחַ, אוֹ זָרַק הַדָּם עַל גַּבֵּי הַמִּזְבֵּחַ, שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמוֹ. שֶׁבְּאַרְבַּע עֲבוֹדוֹת הַלָּלוּ הַמַּחֲשָׁבָה פוֹסֶלֶת הַזֶּבַח:


ששחטו שלא לשמו – such as when they were slaughtered for the sake of peace-offerings, or that the blood was received in the bowl out of which the sprinkling is done or brings the blood to the altar or he cast the blood on the altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:2:2)


**אוֹ לִשְׁמוֹ וְשֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמוֹ.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁשָּׁחַט לִשְׁמוֹ וְקִבֵּל הַדָּם שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמוֹ:


או לשמו ושלא לשמו – such as that it was slaughtered for its sake but received the blood not for its sake.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:2:3)


**אוֹ שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמוֹ.** הַךְ סֵיפָא אַשְׁמְעִינַן דִּמְחַשְּׁבִים מֵעֲבוֹדָה לַעֲבוֹדָה, שֶׁאִם חָשַׁב בִּשְׁעַת עֲבוֹדָה זוֹ עַל חֲבֶרְתָּהּ לַעֲשׂוֹתָהּ בְּמַחֲשָׁבָה פְסוּלָה, כְּגוֹן דְּאָמַר הֲרֵינִי שׁוֹחֵט לִשְׁמוֹ עַל מְנָת לִזְרֹק דָּמוֹ שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמוֹ, נִפְסָל הַזֶּבַח מִיָּד, וַאֲפִלּוּ לֹא עָשָׂה הַשְּׁנִיָּה בְּמַחֲשָׁבָה פְסוּלָה כְּמוֹ שֶׁחָשַׁב. וְהָכִי מַשְׁמָעוּתָא דְמַתְנִיתִין, אוֹ שֶׁחָשַׁב בִּשְׁעַת שְׁחִיטָה לִזְרֹק הַדָּם שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמוֹ וְשָׁחַט לִשְׁמוֹ, פָּסוּל. וּפֶסַח דְּפָסוּל שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמוֹ נָפְקָא לָן מִקְּרָא, דִּכְתִיב (שמות יב) וַאֲמַרְתֶּם זֶבַח פֶּסַח הוּא, עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַזֶּבַח לְשֵׁם פֶּסַח, וְהוּא מַשְׁמַע לְעַכֵּב:


או שלא לשמו – this ending part [of our Mishnah] teaches us that we consider from one form of Divine service to another, for if he thought at the time of the Divine service for this one on the service of its fellow [sacrifice] to perform with that thought, it is invalid, such when he said: “Behold I slaughter for its sake on the condition to cast its blood not for its sake, the sacrifice (i.e., festive peace-offering) is invalid immediately, and even if he didn’t perform the second as an invalid thought like the case as he thought, and that is the implication of our Mishnah, or that he thought at ther time of the slaughter to cast the blood not for its sake, we derive this from the Biblical verse, as it is written (Exodus 12:27): “You shall say, ‘It is the Passover sacrifice to the LORD’…” until it would be that the sacrifice is for the Passover offering and it implies to be indispensable.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:3:1)


**שְׁחָטוֹ שֶׁלֹּא לְאוֹכְלָיו.** חוֹלֶה וְזָקֵן וְקָטָן שֶׁאֵין יְכוֹלִין לֶאֱכֹל כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר, וְאֵין בּוֹ מְנוּיִין אֲחֵרִים אֶלָּא הֵן, פָּסוּל, דִּלְפִי אָכְלוֹ כְּתִיב (שם), הָרָאוּי לֶאֱכֹל:


שחטו שלא לאוכליו – a sick person or an old person or a minor who are unable to eat an olive’s bulk of meat and there aren’t other appointments to office other than these. It is invalid since it is written (Exodus 12:4): “according to what each household will eat.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:3:2)


**שֶׁלֹּא לִמְנוּיָיו.** נִמְנוּ עָלָיו חֲבוּרָה זוֹ וּשְׁחָטוֹ לְשֵׁם חֲבוּרָה אַחֶרֶת:


למנוייו – he was registered for this group and he slaughtered it for another group.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:3:3)


**לַעֲרֵלִים.** לְיִשְׂרְאֵלִים שֶׁמֵּתוּ אֲחֵיהֶם מֵחֲמַת מִילָה, וְהָנֵי פְּסוּלִים לֶאֱכֹל פֶּסַח, דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְכָל עָרֵל לֹא יֹאכַל בּוֹ. וּטְמֵאִים נַמִּי אֲסוּרִין בְּקָדָשִׁים, וּבְכָרֵת הֵן עַל אֲכִילָתָן:


לערלים – For Israelites whose brothers died on account of circumcisions, and these are invalid to eat the Passover sacrifice, as it is written (Exodus 12:48): “But no uncircumcised person may eat of it.” And those who are impure are forbidden to Holy things and they are liable to extirpation on their eating it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:3:4)


**לְאוֹכְלָיו וְשֶׁלֹּא לְאוֹכְלָיו כָּשֵׁר.** וְלֹא דָמִי לִשְׁמוֹ וְשֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמוֹ דְּפָסוּל, דְּהָתָם פְּסוּלוֹ בְגוּפוֹ, שֶׁהַמַּחֲשָׁבָה שֶׁפּוֹסַלְתּוֹ הָיְתָה בְגוּפוֹ שֶׁל קָרְבָּן, אֲבָל לְאוֹכְלָיו וְשֶׁלֹּא לְאוֹכְלָיו אֵין הַמַּחֲשָׁבָה הַפּוֹסֶלֶת בְּגוּפוֹ שֶׁל קָרְבָּן אֶלָּא בְדָבָר שֶׁהוּא חוּץ מִמֶּנּוּ:


לאוכליו ושלא לאוכליו כשר – and it is not similar to for its sake and not for its sake, for invalid that is there – is his being invalid in his body. For the thought that makes him invalid was in the body of the sacrifice, but for those eat it and for those who don’t eat it, the thought does not invalidate ממthe body of the sacrifice, but rather something which is outside of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:3:5)


**מְמָרֵס בְּדָמוֹ.** שֶׁלֹּא יִקְרַשׁ, כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא רָאוּי לִזְרִיקָה:


ממרס בדמו – that the blood not become congealed so that it would be worthy for casting/tossing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:3:6)


**וְאִם זָרַק קֹדֶם לַתָּמִיד כָּשֵׁר.** דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּפֶסַח מְאֻחָר לַתָּמִיד, מִשּׁוּם דִּכְתִיב בֵּיהּ בָּעֶרֶב וּבֵין הָעַרְבַּיִם, לֹא מִפְסַל בְּהָכִי:


ואם זרק קודם לתמיד כשר – since even though the Passover offering is later than the daily whole burnt offering because it is written concerning it (Deuteronomy 16:7 – see Pesahim 59a): “[and none of the flesh of] what you slaughter on the evening…,” and “at twilight” (Exodus 12:6) does not made invalid through this.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:1)


**הַשּׁוֹחֵט אֶת הַפֶּסַח עַל הֶחָמֵץ.** כְּלוֹמַר וּבִשְׁעַת שְׁחִיטָה הָיָה חָמֵץ בִּרְשׁוּת הַשּׁוֹחֵט אוֹ אֶחָד מִבְּנֵי חֲבוּרָה, אֲפִלּוּ לֹא הָיָה הֶחָמֵץ בָּעֲזָרָה:


השוחט את הפסח על החמץ – that is to say, at the time of the slaughtering [of the Passover offering], there was leaven in the domain of the slaughterer or one of the members of his group, even if there was no Hametz in the courtyard [of the Temple]


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:2)


**עוֹבֵר בְּלֹא תַעֲשֶׂה.** דְּלֹא תִשְׁחַט עַל חָמֵץ דַּם זִבְחִי (שמות לד). וְאֵין הַזֶּבַח נִפְסָל:


He violates a negative commandment - of (Exodus 34:25): “You shall not offer the blood of My sacrifice with anything leavened,” and the sacrifice itself is not made invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:3)


**אַף הַתָּמִיד.** שֶׁל בֵּין הָעַרְבַּיִם דְּעַרְבֵי פְסָחִים שֶׁשְּׁחָטוֹ עַל חָמֵץ, שֶׁהָיָה חָמֵץ בִּרְשׁוּת הַשּׁוֹחֵט אוֹ הַמַּקְרִיב, עוֹבֵר בְּלָאו, דְּדָרֵישׁ זִבְחִי, הַמְיֻחָד לִי, וְהוּא הַתָּמִיד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אף תמיד – of the twilight on the Eve of Passover that was slaughtered with leaven that for there was leaven in the domain of the slaughterer or the person offering the sacrifice violates a negative commandment, for he expounds upon the word, “[the blood of] My sacrifice” (Exodus 34:26) that is special to me, and that is the whole daily-offering , but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:4)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר הַפֶּסַח בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר.** שֶׁשְּׁחָטוֹ עַל חָמֵץ, לִשְׁמוֹ חַיָּב, מִשּׁוּם לֹא תִשְׁחַט עַל חָמֵץ, דְּפֶסַח כָּשֵׁר הוּא וּשְׁחִיטָה רְאוּיָה הִיא וּשְׁמָהּ שְׁחִיטָה:


ר' שמעון אומר הפסח בי"ד – that he slaughtered it with leaven for its sake, he is liable because of “You shall not offer the blood of My sacrifice with anything leavened,” (Exodus 34:25) for the Passover Sacrifice is valid/fit and the slaughtering is appropriate and it is for the purpose of slaughtering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:5)


**שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמוֹ פָּטוּר.** דְּפָסוּל הוּא, וּשְׁחִיטָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה הִיא וְלָאו שְׁמָהּ שְׁחִיטָה:


שלא לשמו פטור – that it is invalid and it is a slaughtering that is not worthy and not for the sake of slaughtering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:6)


**וּשְׁאָר כָּל הַזְּבָחִים.** שֶׁנִּשְׁחֲטוּ בְאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר אַחַר חֲצוֹת עַל הֶחָמֵץ:


ושאר כל הזבחים – that were slaughtered on the fourteen [of Nisan] after noon with leaven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:7)


**בֵּין לִשְׁמָן בֵּין שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָן פָּטוּר.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁכְּשֵׁרִים הֵן, כְּדִתְנַן כָּל הַזְּבָחִים שֶׁנִּזְבְּחוּ שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָן כְּשֵׁרִים וְכוּ', אַף עַל פִּי כֵן פָּטוּר, דִּכְתִיב זִבְחִי זִבְחִי שְׁתֵּי פְעָמִים, לֹא תִשְׁחַט עַל חָמֵץ דַּם זִבְחִי וְלֹא תִזְבַּח עַל חָמֵץ דַּם זִבְחִי (שם כג), וּלְמַאי הִלְכְתָא פַּלְגִינְהוּ רַחֲמָנָא וְלֹא כְתַב זְבָחַי בְּחַד קְרָא וְהַכֹּל בְּמַשְׁמָע בֵּין פֶּסַח בֵּין שְׁאָר זְבָחִים, לוֹמַר לְךָ בִּזְמַן דְּאִיכָּא זֶבַח, דְּהַיְנוּ בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר דְּאִיכָּא פֶסַח, לֹא מִחַיַּב אַשְּׁאָר זְבָחִים, וּבִזְמַן דְּלֵיכָּא זֶבַח, כְּגוֹן בְּתוֹךְ הַפֶּסַח, מִחַיַּב אַשְּׁאָר זְבָחִים אִם שְׁחָטָן עַל חָמֵץ:


בין לשמן בין שלא לשמן פטור – for even though they are fit, as it is taught in the Mishnah (Tractate Zevakhim, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1 AND Tractate Yadayim, Chapter 4, Mishnah 2): “All the animal offerings that were slaughtered not for their own name are valid, etc.” nevertheless, they are exempt, as it is written: (Exodus 34:25): “My sacrifice,” “My sacrifice” twice: “You shall not offer the blood of My sacrifice [with anything leavened].” (Exodus 24:25) and (Exodus 23:18): ‘You shall not offer the blood of My sacrifice with anything leavened.” And what Halakha did the All-Merciful separate them and not write “My sacrifice” in one verse? And everything is implied, whether regarding the Passover offering or regarding all the rest of the animal sacrifices, to tell you that at the time when there is an animal sacrifice that is, on the fourteenth [of Nisan] when there is the Passover offering, he is not liable for the rest of the animal sacrifices, and at the time when there isn’t an animal sacrifice, such as during the Passover, he is liable for the other animal sacrifices if they were slaughtered with leaven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:8)


**וּבַמּוֹעֵד.** בְּתוֹךְ הַפֶּסַח, אִם לִשְׁמוֹ שְׁחָטוֹ לַפֶּסַח עַל הֶחָמֵץ:


ובמועד – during the Passover holiday, if it was for the sake/in its name that it was slaughtered for the Passover offering with leaven,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:9)


**פָּטוּר.** מִלֹּא תִשְׁחַט, דְּפֶסַח שֶׁלֹּא בִזְמַנּוֹ לִשְׁמוֹ פָּסוּל, וְהַוְיָא לַהּ שְׁחִיטָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה:


it is exempt from “You shall not offer [the blood of My sacrifice with anything leavened]” (Exodus 34:25), for a Passover offering that was not at its appropriate time, for its sake, is invalid, and it is a slaughtering that is not appropriate.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:10)


**שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמוֹ.** אֶלָּא לְשֵׁם שְׁלָמִים:


שלא לשמו – but for the sake of peace-offerings,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:11)


**חַיָּב.** דִּלְהָכִי קָאֵי, וְהַוְיָא לַהּ שְׁחִיטָה רְאוּיָה, וְחַיָּב מִשּׁוּם לֹא תִשְׁחַט, בַּהֲדֵי לָאו דְּלֹא יֵרָאֶה וְלֹא יִמָּצֵא:


one is liable, for this is what it refers to that it should be the appropriate slaughtering, and he is liable because of “You shall not offer,” with the negative commandment of (Exodus 13:7), “no leaven shall be seen with you” and (Exodus 12:19): “no leaven shall be found in your houses for seven days.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:12)


**וּשְׁאָר כָּל הַזְּבָחִים.** שֶׁנִּזְבְּחוּ בְּתוֹךְ הַמּוֹעֵד עַל חָמֵץ:


ושאר כל הזבחים – that were slaughtered during the Festival [of Passover] with leaven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:13)


**בֵּין לִשְׁמָן בֵּין שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָן חַיָּב.** דִּקְרָא אַשְּׁאָר זְבָחִים נַמִּי אַזְהַר:


בין לשמן בין שלא לשמן חייב – for the Biblical verse on the rest of the offerings is also a warning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:4:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:4:14)


**חוּץ מִן הַחַטָּאת שֶׁשְּׁחָטָהּ שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָהּ.** דִּפְסוּלָה הִיא, מִשּׁוּם דִּכְתִיב בִּשְׁחִיטָתָהּ חַטָּאת הִיא, הִיא וְלֹא הַנִּשְׁחֲטָה שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָהּ:


חוץ מן החטאת ששחטה שלא לשמה – and it is invalid because it is written regarding the slaughtering of a sin-offering, it and not what was slaughtered not for its own sake.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:5:1)


**הַפֶּסַח נִשְׁחָט בְּשָׁלֹשׁ כִּתּוֹת.** [בֵּין שֶׁהַצִּבּוּר מְרֻבִּים], בֵּין שֶׁהַצִּבּוּר מֻעָטִים וִיכוֹלִים כֻּלָּן לִשְׁחֹט בְּבַת אַחַת, מִצְוָה לֵחָלֵק לְשָׁלֹשׁ כִּתּוֹת זוֹ אַחַר זוֹ:


הפסח נשחט בשלש כתות – [whether the community is large/many] whether the community is small, and all of them would be able to slaughter at the same time, it is Mitzvah to divide up into three groups one after the other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:5:2)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר וְשָׁחֲטוּ אֹתוֹ כָּל קְהַל עֲדַת יִשְׂרָאֵל.** קָהָל, וְעֵדָה, וְיִשְׂרָאֵל. הֲרֵי שָׁלֹשׁ כִּתּוֹת:


שנאמר ושחטו אותו כל קהל עדת ישראל – “assembled,” and “congregation” and “of Israel” , these are three groups.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:5:3)


**בָּזִיכֵי.** כַּפּוֹת גְּדוֹלוֹת לְקַבֵּל בָּהֶם הַדָּם:


בזיכי – large pans/censers to receive the blood in them .


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:5:4)


**שׁוּרָה שֶׁכֻּלָּהּ כֶּסֶף כֶּסֶף.** דְּהָכִי שַׁפִּיר טְפֵי:


שורה שכולה כסף כסף – and this is much better.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:5:5)


**וְלֹא הָיוּ לַבָּזִיכִים שׁוּלַיִם.** רְחָבִים הָיוּ מִלְמַעְלָה וְתַחְתֵּיהֶם חַדִּים, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יוּכְלוּ לֵישֵׁב עַל הַקַּרְקַע:


ולא היו לבזיכים שלוים – wide, sharp [rims/saucers] from above and below them in order that they wouldn’t be able to it on the ground lest the Kohanim place them to sit on the ground, until they receive other blood on account that they are any and they might forget them and the blood will congeal and it won’t be appropriate for tossing/casting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:5:6)


**שֶׁמָּא יַנִּיחוּם.** הַכֹּהֲנִים לֵישֵׁב עַל הַקַּרְקַע עַד שֶׁיְּקַבֵּל דָּם אַחֵר, מֵחֲמַת שֶׁהֵם מְרֻבִּים, וְיִשְׁכְּחוּם וְיִקְרַשׁ הַדָּם וְלֹא יִהְיֶה רָאוּי לִזָּרֵק:



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:1)


**שָׁחַט יִשְׂרָאֵל.** אִם יִרְצֶה, שֶׁהַשְּׁחִיטָה כְשֵׁרָה בְזָר בְּכָל הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת:


שחט ישראל – if he wanted hat the slaughtering would be fit by a foreigner (i.e., non-Kohen) for all of the sacrifices.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:2)


**וְקִבֵּל כֹּהֵן.** הַדָּם בַּבָּזֵךְ, שֶׁמִּקַּבָּלָה וָאֵילָךְ מִצְוַת כְּהֻנָּה:


וקבל הכהן – [The Kohen received] the blood in the vessel/censer – that when he receives it and onwards is the Mitzvah for the priesthood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:3)


**נוֹתְנוֹ לַחֲבֵרוֹ.** שֶׁהֲרֵי כְּשׁוּרָה הֵן עוֹמְדִים:


נותנו לחברו – for they are standing like a line.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:4)


**וַחֲבֵרוֹ לַחֲבֵרוֹ.** הָא קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דִּבְרֹב עַם הַדְרַת מֶלֶךְ:


וחברו לחברו – for it comes to teach us that (Proverbs 14:28): “A numerous people is the glory of the king.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:5)


**וּמְקַבֵּל אֶת הַמָּלֵא.** מִיַּד הַנּוֹתֵן, תְּחִלָּה, וְאַחַר כָּךְ מַחֲזִיר אֶת הָרֵיקָן. אֲבָל אִפְּכָא, מַחֲזִיר אֶת הָרֵיקָן בְּרֵישָׁא לֹא, דְּמִכִּי מוֹשִׁיט לֵיהּ הַאי מִיָּד בָּעֵי לְקַבּוֹלֵי, דְּאֵין מַעֲבִירִין עַל הַמִּצְוֹת:


ומקבל את המלא – from the hand of the one gives first and afterwards, he returns the empty one, but not the opposite – that he returns the empty one first, for when he extends this to him from his hand it is required to receive it for we cannot forgo the opportunity to observe the commandments.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:6)


**זְרִיקָה אַחַת.** בַּבָּזֵךְ עַצְמוֹ, וְלֹא מַתָּנָה בְאֶצְבַּע, שֶׁאֵין לְךָ קָרְבָּן שֶׁטָּעוּן אֶצְבַּע אֶלָּא חַטָּאת לְבַדָּהּ דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ד) בְּאֶצְבָּעוֹ:


זריקה אחת – in the vessel/censer itself and not a gift with the finger, for you do not have a sacrifice that requires a finger other than the sin-offering itself, as it is written (Leviticus 4:30): “[The priest shall take] with his finger [some of its blood and put it on the horns of the altar of the burnt offering].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:6:7)


**כְּנֶגֶד הַיְסוֹד.** בָּרוּחוֹת שֶׁבַּמִּזְבֵּחַ שֶׁהַיְסוֹד תַּחְתֵּיהֶן, וְזוֹרֵק לִזְקִיפָתוֹ שֶׁל מִזְבֵּחַ וְהוּא נוֹפֵל לַיְסוֹד. וּלְפִי שֶׁאֵין הַיְסוֹד מַקִּיף כָּל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ אֶלָּא אֶת הַצָּפוֹן וְאֶת הַמַּעֲרָב, וְאוֹכֵל בַּדָּרוֹם אַמָּה אַחַת וּבַמִּזְרָח אַמָּה אַחַת כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת מִדּוֹת, לְכָךְ הֻצְרַךְ לוֹמַר נֶגֶד הַיְסוֹד:


כנגד היסוד – in the directions of that are on the altar that the base is below them and he casts while he is upright of the altar and he falls to the base, and because the base does not surround the altar other than the northern side and the western side and he eats on the southern side one cubit and on the east side one cubit as it is explained in the Tractate Middot (Chapter 3, Mishnah 1), therefore it was necessary to say “toward the base.”



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:7:1)


**קָרְאוּ אֶת הַהַלֵּל.** כָּל הַשָּׁלֹשׁ כִּתּוֹת:


קראו את ההלל – each of the three groups.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:7:2)


**אִם גָּמְרוּ שָׁנוּ.** כְּשֶׁהָיוּ מַתְחִילִים לִשְׁחֹט הָיוּ מַתְחִילִים לִקְרוֹת, וְאִם רַבּוּ הַפְּסָחִים וּמָשַׁךְ זְמַן שְׁחִיטָתָן עַד שֶׁגָּמְרוּ וַעֲדַיִן רַבִּים לִשְׁחֹט, חוֹזְרִים וְקוֹרִין שְׁנִיָּה. וְאִם קָרְאוּ שְׁנִיָּה וַעֲדַיִן לֹא גָמְרוּ שְׁחִיטַת הַפְּסָחִים שֶׁל אוֹתָהּ הַכַּת, חוֹזְרִין וְקוֹרִין שְׁלִישִׁית. וְכֵן כַּת שְׁנִיָּה וּשְׁלִישִׁית:


אם גמרו שנו – When they would begin to slaughter, they would begin to recite [Hallel] and if there many Passover sacrifices and the time of their slaughtering stretched out until they completed and still there were many to slaughter, they would return and recite [Hallel] a second time and if they read it twice and still had not completed the slaughtering of the Passover offerings of that group, they would go back and recite it a third time, and similarly for the second group and the third group.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:7:3)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמֵּעוֹלָם לֹא שִׁלְּשׁוּ.** שֶׁהַכֹּהֲנִים הָיוּ רַבִּים וּזְרִיזִין:


אע"פ שמעולם לא שלשו – for the Kohanim were many and zealous.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:7:4)


**לֹא הִגִּיעוּ לְאָהַבְתִּי.** אֲפִלּוּ פַּעַם רִאשׁוֹנָה:


לא הגיעו לאהבתי – even one time.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:8:1)


**שֶׁהַכֹּהֲנִים הָיוּ מְדִיחִין אֶת הָעֲזָרָה.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַדָּמִים מְרֻבִּים הָיוּ מְדִיחִין אוֹתָהּ בְּשַׁבָּת. שֶׁאַמַּת הַמַּיִם הָיְתָה מְהַלֶּכֶת בָּעֲזָרָה, וּכְשֶׁהֵן רוֹצִים לְהָדִיחַ פּוֹקְקִים אֶת נֶקֶב יְצִיאָתָהּ וְהַמַּיִם פּוֹשְׁטִים עַל כָּל גְּדוֹתֶיהָ וּמְדִיחִין כָּל הָעֲזָרָה [שֶׁהָעֲזָרָה רִצְפָּה שֶׁל שַׁיִשׁ הָיְתָה כֻלָּהּ], וְאַחַר כָּךְ פּוֹתְחִין הַנֶּקֶב וְהַמַּיִם יוֹצְאִים:


שהכהנים מדיחים את העזרה – because there was a great amount of blood , they would rinse it (i.e., the courtyard) on the Sabbath for the canal of water would go in the courtyard and when they would want to rinse it, they would stop up the hole where it (i.e., the water) comes out and the water stretches out over its banks and rinses the entire courtyard [for the courtyard was entirely a floor of alabaster], and afterwards they open the hole and the waters come out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:8:2)


**מִדַּם הַתַּעֲרֹבוֹת.** הַמֻּטָּל עַל הָרִצְפָּה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


מדם התערובות – that is lying on the floor. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:9:1)


**אֻנְקְלָיוֹת.** מַסְמֵרִים שֶׁרָאשֵׁיהֶן כְּפוּפִים לְמַעְלָה:


אונקליות – nails whose heads were bent from above.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:9:2)


**וּבָעַמּוּדִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ קְבוּעִין בְּבֵית הַמִּטְבָּחַיִם בָּעֲזָרָה עַמּוּדִים קְטַנִּים נִקְרָאִים נַנָּסִים:


ובעמודים – that were set up in the slaughter house, in the courtyard were small pillars that were called “stumped”/”dwarfed” (see Mishnah Tamid, Chapter 3, Mishnah 5).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:9:3)


**חֲלָקִים.** מְפֻצֶּלֶת קְלִפָּתָן:


חלקים – their scales/skin was branched off/forked.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:9:4)


**שֶׁחָל לִהְיוֹת בְּשַׁבָּת.** וְאֵין יָכוֹל לְטַלְטֵל הַמַּקְלוֹת:


שחל להיות בשבת – and they are not able to carry the poles.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:9:5)


**יָדוֹ עַל כֶּתֶף חֲבֵרוֹ.** וְתוֹלֶה אוֹתוֹ בְּגִידֵי אַרְכֻּבּוֹתָיו בִּזְרוֹעוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר, שֶׁמֻּתָּר לְטַלְטֵל אוֹתָן הַמַּקְלוֹת, דְּאֵין שְׁבוּת בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ:


ידו על כתף חבירו – and they hang it on the membrum of his knees with his arm. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer, for it is permitted to carry the poles since there is no prohibition of acts as Sh’vut (forbidden by the Rabbis as out of harmony with the celebration of the day) in the Temple (see Pesahim 65a).



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:10:1)


**אֵמוּרָיו.** הַחֲלָבִים הַקְּרֵבִים עַל גַּבֵּי הַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


אימוריו – the fats that were offered on the altar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:10:2)


**בְּמָגִיס.** בִּקְעָרָה. תַּרְגּוּם קַעֲרוֹתָיו מָגִיסוֹהִי:


במגיס – a bowl; the Aramaic translation of his bowls is “his trays/his bowls.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:10:3)


**יָשְׁבָה לָהּ בְּהַר הַבַּיִת.** בְּשַׁבָּת קָאָמַר, שֶׁאֵין יְכוֹלִין לְהוֹלִיךְ פִּסְחֵיהֶן:


ישבה לה בהר הבית – It is speaking about Shabbat when one cannot bring up their Passover offerings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:10:4)


**בַּחֵיל.** בֵּין הַסּוֹרֵג לְחוֹמַת הָעֲזָרָה שֶׁל נָשִׁים בִּתְחִלַּת עֲלִיַּת הַבַּיִת:


בחיל – between the lattice-work (name of one of the approaches of the Temple fortification – see Mishnah Middot, Chapter 2, Mishnah 3) and the wall of the women’s court at the beginning of the ascent to the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 5:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/5:10:5)


**חֲשֵׁכָה יָצְאוּ וְצָלוּ אֶת פִּסְחֵיהֶן.** שֶׁאֵין צְלִיַּת הַפֶּסַח דּוֹחָה אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת:


חשיכה יצאו וצלו את פסחיהן – for the roasting of the Passover offering does not override the [prohibitions of the] Sabbath.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:1:1)
