**אֵלּוּ דְבָרִים בַּפֶּסַח דּוֹחִין אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת.** פֶּסַח דּוֹחֶה אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת, דְּנֶאֱמַר מוֹעֲדוֹ בַּפֶּסַח (במדבר ט) וְיַעֲשׂוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת הַפָּסַח בְּמוֹעֲדוֹ, וְנֶאֱמַר מוֹעֲדוֹ בַּתָּמִיד (שם כח) תִּשְׁמְרוּ לְהַקְרִיב לִי בְּמוֹעֲדוֹ, מַה מּוֹעֲדוֹ הָאָמוּר בַּתָּמִיד דּוֹחֶה אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שם) עַל עוֹלַת הַתָּמִיד, אַף מוֹעֲדוֹ הָאָמוּר בַּפֶּסַח דּוֹחֶה אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת:


ואלו דברים בפסח דוחין את השבת – The Passover offering overrides the [prohibitions of the] Sabbath, as it states that its set time is Passover (Numbers 9:2): “Let the Israelite people offer the Passover sacrifice at its set time.” And it (i.e., the Torah) states “set time” with relation to the daily whole burnt-offering (Numbers 28:2): “Be punctilious in presenting to Me at stated times…”Just as “its set time” which is stated in regard to the whole daily burnt-offering overrides the [prohibitions of the] Sabbath, as it is stated (Numbers 28:15): “in addition to the regular burnt offering,” so also “its set time” that is stated in connection to the Passover offering overrides the [prohibitions of the] Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:1:2)


**שְׁחִיטָתוֹ וּזְרִיקַת דָּמוֹ.** אִי אֶפְשָׁר לַעֲשׂוֹתָן בַּלַּיְלָה, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ז) בְּיוֹם צַוֹּתוֹ לְהַקְרִיב, בַּיּוֹם וְלֹא בַלַּיְלָה:


שחיטתו וזריקת דמו – it is impossible to perform them at night, as it is written (Leviticus 7:38): “when [during the day] He commanded that the Israelites present their offerings to the LORD…” during the day, and not during the nighttime.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:1:3)


**מִחוּי.** קִנּוּחַ, כְּמוֹ אָכְלָה וּמָחֲתָה פִּיהָ (משלי ל). מִחוּי קְרָבָיו דּוֹחֶה אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת, שֶׁלֹּא יַסְרִיחוּ:


מחוי – wiping, like (Proverbs 30:20): “She eats, wipes her mouth.” Scraping its entrails overrides the Sabbath, so that it does not smell badly.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:1:4)


**צְלִיָּתוֹ וַהֲדָחַת קְרָבָיו אֵינָן דּוֹחִין.** דְּאֶפְשָׁר מִשֶּׁתֶּחְשַׁךְ:


צלייתו והדחת קרביו אין דוחין – for it is possible [to do these] after it becomes dark.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:1:5)


**הַרְכָּבָתוֹ.** עַל כְּתֵפוֹ לַהֲבִיאוֹ דֶּרֶךְ רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לָעֲזָרָה, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ אֶלָּא אִסּוּר שְׁבוּת, שֶׁהַחַי נוֹשֵׂא אֶת עַצְמוֹ, אֵין דּוֹחֶה, דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ לְמֶעְבְּדַהּ מֵאֶתְמוֹל. וְכֵן הֲבָאָתוֹ מִחוּץ לַתְּחוּם וַחֲתִיכַת יַבַּלְתּוֹ שֶׁל קָרְבָּן פֶּסַח לְהָסִיר מוּמוֹ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ אֶלָּא אִסּוּר שְׁבוּת, שֶׁחוֹתְכָהּ בְּשִׁנָּיו אוֹ בְיָדוֹ, אֵינָן דּוֹחִים. וְהָא דִתְנַן בְּשִׁלְהֵי עֵרוּבִין חוֹתְכִין יַבֶּלֶת בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ, הַהִיא בִּיבֵשָׁה דְּמִפְרָךְ פַּרְכָא, וּמַתְנִיתִין דְּהָכָא בְּלַחָה. וְאַף עַל פִּי דִּכְשֶׁחוֹתְכָהּ בְּשִׁנָּיו אוֹ בְיָדוֹ כִּלְאַחַר יָד הוּא, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי אָסוּר בְּלַחָה, דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ לְמֶעְבְּדַהּ מֵאֶתְמוֹל:


הרכבתו – on his shoulder to bring it via the public domain to the Temple courtyard, and even though there is no prohibition other than Sh’vut/an occupation forbidden by the Rabbis on the Sabbath as being out of harmony with the celebration of the day for the living can carry itself, it does not override [the prohibitions of the Sabbath day] for he could have prepared it from yesterday, and similarly, his bringing it from outside the Sabbath limits, and the cutting of its (i.e., the sacrifice’s) warts of the Passover offering to remove its defects even though he has no prohibition other than Sh’vut, that he cuts it with his teeth or with his hand, they do not override the prohibitions of the Sabbath. And as it is taught in the Mishnah at the end of Tractate Eruvin (Chapter 10, Mishnah 13), “They cut off a wart in the Temple,” this is something dry, that one crushes that which is brittle but our Mishnah here is speaking of something moist (Pesahim 68b), and even though he severs it with his teeth or with his hand, or indirectly , and even as such, it is prohibited with a moist [wart], for he could have done It from yesterday.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:1:6)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר דּוֹחִין.** דְּסָבַר מַכְשִׁירֵי מִצְוָה דּוֹחִים אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת, בְּמַסֶּכֶת שַׁבָּת:


דוחין – for he (i.e., Rabbi Eliezer) holds that requirements for a religious act [to be performed on the Sabbath] override the observance of Shabbat (see Shabbat 19a).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:1)


**שְׁחִיטָה שֶׁהִיא.** אֲסוּרָה בְחֻלִּין בְּשַׁבָּת מִשּׁוּם אִסּוּר מְלָאכָה גְמוּרָה, אַף עַל פִּי כֵן דּוֹחָה אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת בַּפֶּסַח:


שחיטה שהיא – [slaughter which is] forbidden with non-holy animals on the Sabbath because of the complete prohibition of work, even though they override the [prohibitions of the] Sabbath on Passover.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:2)


**אֵלּוּ שֶׁהֵם מִשּׁוּם שְׁבוּת לֹא יִדְחוּ.** בִּתְמִיהָה:


אל שהם משום שבות לא ידחו – with astonishment!


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:3)


**יוֹם טוֹב יוֹכִיחַ.** שֶׁהִתִּירוּ בוֹ שְׁחִיטָה וּבִשּׁוּל שֶׁהוּא אַב מְלָאכָה, וּמֻתָּרִין לְהֶדְיוֹט, וְאָסְרוּ בוֹ לְהָבִיא דָבָר מִחוּץ לַתְּחוּם וּלְאָכְלוֹ, הוֹאִיל וְהָיָה אֶפְשָׁר לוֹ מֵאֶתְמוֹל, וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּתְחוּמִים מִדְּרַבָּנָן:


יום טוב יוכיח – that they permitted on it (i.e., Yom Tov) slaughtering and cooking which is one of the primary forms of work and are permitted to the commoner, but they prohibited on it to bring something from outside to the Sabbath limits and to eat it, since it was possible for him [to do so] from the day before, and even though the Sabbath limits are Rabbinic [in nature and origin].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:4)


**מָה רְאָיָה רְשׁוּת לְמִצְוָה.** אֲכִילַת הֶדְיוֹט רְשׁוּת הִיא, וְצֹרֶךְ גָּבוֹהַּ מִצְוָה הִיא, וְאִם הֶעֱמִידוּ חֲכָמִים אִסּוּר שְׁבוּת שֶׁלָּהֶן בִּמְקוֹם רְשׁוּת, יַעֲמִידוּהוּ אַף בִּמְקוֹם מִצְוָה. וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ סָבַר כָּל שִׂמְחַת יוֹם טוֹב מִצְוָה הִיא, וַאֲפִלּוּ הָכִי אֵינָהּ דּוֹחָה שְׁבוּת:


מה ראיה רשות למצוה – the eating of a commoner is optional, but for the needs of On High (i.e., God), it is a commandment, and if the Sages established the prohibition of their Sh’vut/an occupation forbidden as being out of harmony with the celebration of the day in an optional place, they can establish it even in the place of a commandment/Mitzvah. And Rabbi Yehoshua holds that every joy of the Yom Tov/Festial is a commandment, but nevertheless, it does override Sh’vut.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:5)


**הַזָּאָה תוֹכִיחַ שֶׁהִיא מִצְוָה.** בִּטְמֵא מֵת שֶׁחָל שְׁבִיעִי שֶׁלּוֹ לִהְיוֹת בְּשַׁבַּת עֶרֶב הַפֶּסַח, שֶׁאִם לֹא יַזֶּה לֹא יַעֲשֶׂה פִסְחוֹ, וַאֲפִלּוּ הָכִי אֵינוֹ דוֹחֶה, דְּהָכִי קִים לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא דְּלֹא דַּחְיָא. וְהַזָּאָה שְׁבוּת הִיא, דְּמֵחֲזֵי כְּמָאן דִּמְתַקֵּן גַּבְרָא:


הזאה תוכיח שהיא מצוה – with someone who is defiled through contact with the dead that his seventh days occurs on the Sabbath on the Eve of Passover, for if he would not sprinkle, he could not offer his Passover sacrifice, and nevertheless, it does not override [the prohibitions of Shabbat] for Rabbi Akiva has established/holds it does not override [the prohibitions of Shabbat]. For the sprinkling [of the blood] is Sh’vut/an occupation forbidden as begin out of harmony with the celebration of the day, for it appears as something that a man does repair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:6)


**וְעָלֶיהָ אֲנִי דָן.** וְגַם עַל הַהַזָּאָה אֲנִי חוֹלֵק וְאוֹמַר שֶׁתִּדְחֶה וְלֹא יְעַכְּבֵהוּ מִפֶּסַח, מִקַּל וָחֹמֶר זֶה בְּעַצְמוֹ:


ועליה אני דן – and also on the sprinkling [of the blood] I dispute and say that it should override [the prohibitions of the Sabbath] and is not indispensable from the Passover sacrifice, from an inference from the weaker to the stronger/Kal VaHomer on its own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:7)


**אָמַר לוֹ רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹ חִלּוּף.** אוֹ אֲנִי אַחֲלִיף אֶת הַדִּין. דִּפְשִׁיטָא לֵיהּ דְּהַזָּאָה מְעַכְּבָא, וְיָלְפִינַן בְּקַל וָחֹמֶר מִנַּהּ לִשְׁחִיטָה [שֶׁתְּעַכֵּב]:


א"ל ר' עקיבא או חלוף – or I will reverse the law, for it is obvious to him that sprinkling [of the blood[ is indispensable and we derive it by use of a Kal VaHomer/an inference from the weaker to the stronger from it to the slaughtering [which is indispensable].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:8)


**בְּמֹעֲדוֹ.** וְיַעֲשׂוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת הַפָּסַח בְּמוֹעֲדוֹ (במדבר ט):


במועדו – “Let the Israelite people offer the Passover sacrifice at its set time” (Numbers 9:2).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:2:9)


**הָבֵא לִי מוֹעֵד לָאֵלּוּ.** שֶׁיְּהֵא לָהֶם זְמַן קָבוּעַ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁקֶּבַע לַשְּׁחִיטָה, הִלְכָּךְ כֵּיוָן שֶׁלֹּא קָבַע לָהֶם זְמַן וּמָצֵי לְמֶעֱבַד מֵאֶתְמוֹל, לֹא דַחְיָא, וְהַזָּאָה נַמִּי לָאו מִגּוּפֵיהּ דְּפֶסַח הִיא, וְלֹא כְתִיב בֵּיהּ בְּמוֹעֲדוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


הבא לי מועד לאלו – that they should have a fixed time, just as they fixed for slaughtering, therefore, since they did not fix a time for them and it is able to be done from the day before, it does not override [the prohibitions of Shabbat]; but the sprinkling [of the blood] is not from the essence of the Passover sacrifice and the words, “at its set time” are not written concerning it. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:3:1)


**אֵימָתַי מְבִיאִים עִמּוֹ חֲגִיגָה.** עִם הַפֶּסַח בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר בְּנִיסָן:


אימתי מביאים עמו חגיגה – with the Passover offering on the fourteenth of Nisan.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:3:2)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהוּא בָא בְּחֹל וּבְטָהֳרָה.** דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּפֶסַח דָּחֵי שַׁבָּת וְטֻמְאָה, חֲגִיגָה לֹא דַחְיָא שַׁבָּת:


בשמן שהוא בא בחול ובטהרה – for even though that the Passover offering overrides the [prohibitions of the] Sabbath and impurity, the [free-will] Festival offering does not override the [prohibitions of the] Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:3:3)


**וּבְמֻעָט.** שֶׁהָיָה פֶסַח מֻעָט לַאֲכִילַת בְּנֵי חֲבוּרָה, וְאוֹכְלִין הַחֲגִיגָה תְחִלָּה כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַפֶּסַח גְּמַר שְׂבִיעָתָן:


ובמועט – if the Passover offering was too small for the feeding of the members of the group, and they consume the Festival offering first in order that the Passover offering would be the completion of their satisfaction.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:3:4)


**בִּמְרֻבֶּה.** שֶׁהָיְתָה הַחֲבוּרָה מֻעֶטֶת וְדַי לָהֶן בַּפֶּסַח לְבַדּוֹ:


במרובה – that the group was small and the Passover offering was enough for them alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:3:5)


**וּבְטֻמְאָה.** שֶׁרֹב צִבּוּר טְמֵאִין:


ובטומאה – that most of the community is ritually impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:3:6)


**אֵין מְבִיאִין עִמּוֹ חֲגִיגָה.** שֶׁחֲגִיגַת יוֹם אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר רְשׁוּת הִיא וְלֹא חוֹבָה:


אין מביאין עמו חגיגה – as the Festive offering of the fourteenth is option and not obligatory.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:4:1)


**מִן הַבָּקָר.** מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן בַּפֶּסַח, שֶׁאֵינוֹ בָא לֹא מִן הַבָּקָר וְלֹא מִן הַנְּקֵבוֹת:


מן הבקר – which is not the case on Passover that it doesn’t come neither from the cattle and not from the female [sheep].



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:1)


**הַפֶּסַח שֶׁשְּׁחָטוֹ שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמוֹ.** בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר שֶׁחָל לִהְיוֹת בְּשַׁבָּת, וְסָבוּר כְּשֵׁם שֶׁמֻּתָּר לִשְׁמוֹ כָּךְ מֻתָּר שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמוֹ:


הפסח ששחטו שלא לשמו – on the fourteenth of Nisan that fell on Shabbat and thinking that just as it is permissible for the sake of those who eat it, similarly it is permissible for the sake of those who may not eat it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:2)


**חַיָּב חַטָּאת.** שֶׁחִלֵּל שַׁבָּת בְּשׁוֹגֵג:


חייב חטאת – for he violated the Sabbath inadvertently.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:3)


**אִם אֵינָן רְאוּיִין.** לְפֶסַח, כְּגוֹן עֵגֶל אוֹ אַיִל בֶּן שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים אוֹ נְקֵבָה, חַיָּב עָלָיו חַטָּאת אִם נִתְעַלְּמָה מִמֶּנּוּ שַׁבָּת אוֹ סָבוּר שֶׁמֻּתָּר לִשְׁחֹט זְבָחִים אֲחֵרִים לְשֵׁם פֶּסַח בְּשַׁבָּת, דְּהַאי לָאו טוֹעֶה בִדְבַר מִצְוָה, דְּהַכֹּל יוֹדְעִים שֶׁאֵין זֶה כָּשֵׁר לְפֶסַח:


אם אינן ראויין – for the Passover offering, such a calf or a ram that is two years old or a female, he is obligated upon it a sin-offering if escaped his memory that it was Shabbat, or that we was permitted to slaughter other sacrifices for the purpose of the Passover offering on Shabbat, for he is not erring in the mater of a commandment, for everyone knows that that it is not fit for Passover.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:4)


**וְאִם רְאוּיִין הֵן.** כְּגוֹן שֶׂה בֶּן שָׁנָה שֶׁל שְׁלָמִים שֶׁשְּׁחָטוֹ לְשֵׁם פֶּסַח, דְּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהוּא טָרוּד וּבָהוּל לִשְׁחֹט פִּסְחוֹ טָעָה בְדָבָר זֶה וְלֹא נִזְכַּר שֶׁהִקְדִּישׁוֹ לְזֶבַח אַחֵר:


ואם ראויין הן – such as a lamb for a peace offering that was slaughtered for the purpose of the Passover offering, and because he was hasty and busily engaged to slaughter his Passover offering, he erred in this matter and did not remember that it been sanctified for another offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:5)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מְחַיֵּב חַטָּאת.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁטָּעָה בִדְבַר מִצְוָה:


ר"א מחייב חטאת – even though he erred in the matter of a commandment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:6)


**וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ פּוֹטֵר.** דְּקָסָבַר טָעָה בִדְבַר מִצְוָה וְעָשָׂה מִצְוָה כָּל דְּהוּא פָּטוּר מֵחִיּוּב חַטָּאת שֶׁבָּהּ, וְזֶה עָשָׂה מִצְוָה שֶׁהִקְרִיב קָרְבָּן, דְּכָל הַזְּבָחִים שֶׁנִּזְבְּחוּ שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָן כְּשֵׁרִים, וְאַף הַנִּשְׁחָטִים לְשֵׁם פֶּסַח מַכְשִׁיר לְהוּ רַבִּי יְהוּדָה בְּפֶרֶק תָּמִיד נִשְׁחָט:


ור'י פוטר – for he holds that he erred in the matter of a commandment and performed a commandment on this account, he is exempt from the liability of a sin-offering connected with it , for he did the Mitzvah by offering a sacrifice, and all the offerings that were made not for the sake of that offering are valid, and even if they are slaughtered for the purpose of the Passover offering, Rabbi Yehuda declares them fit in the chapter “The daily whole-burnt offering that was slaughtered” (Mishnah Pesahim, Chapter 5, Mishnah 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:7)


**מָה אִם הַפֶּסַח.** שֶׁמֻּתָּר לְשָׁחְטוֹ בְשַׁבָּת לִשְׁמוֹ, שִׁנָּה אֶת שְׁמוֹ אוֹדֵית מִיהָא דְּחַיָּב חַטָּאת כִּדְקָתָנֵי לְעֵיל:


מה אם פסח – that one is permitted to slaughter it (i.e., the Passover sacrifice) on Shabbat for its own sake, if he changed its sake, you have admitted, at least, that you are liable for a sin-offering as is taught above.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:8)


**שֶׁשִּׁנָּהוּ לְדָבָר הָאָסוּר.** שֶׁשְּׁחָטוֹ לְשֵׁם קָרְבָּנוֹת אֲחֵרִים שֶׁאֲסוּרִים לְשָׁחְטָן בְּשַׁבָּת:


ששינהו לדבר האסור – for he slaughtered it for the sake of other sacrifices that are prohibited to slaughter them for on Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:9)


**שֶׁשִּׁנָּהוּ לְדָבָר הַמֻּתָּר.** שֶׁשּׁוֹחֲטוֹ לְשֵׁם דָּבָר הַמֻּתָּר לְשָׁחְטוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת:


ששינהו לדבר המותר – that he slaughtered it for the purpose of something that is permitted to slaughter it on Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:10)


**אֵמוּרֵי צִבּוּר.** קָרְבָּנוֹת הָאֲמוּרִים בְּצִבּוּר בְּשַׁבָּת כְּגוֹן תְּמִידִים וּמוּסָפִים יוֹכִיחוּ, שֶׁמֻּתָּרִים לְשָׁחְטָן לִשְׁמָן, וְהַשּׁוֹחֵט שְׁאָר זְבָחִים לִשְׁמָן בְּשַׁבָּת חַיָּב:


אימורי צבור – the parts of those sacrifices offered on the altar in public on the Sabbath such as the daily whole-offerings and the additional/Musaf offerings will prove that they are permitted to slaughter them for their own sake. And he who slaughters other sacrifices for the sake of their specific purpose on the Sabbath is liable.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:11)


**שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶן קִצְבָּה.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ רוֹאֶה אֲחֵרִים עֲסוּקִין בִּשְׁחִיטָתוֹ, וְכֵיוָן שֶׁנִּשְׁחַט הַתָּמִיד יוֹדֵעַ הוּא שֶׁאֵין צָרִיךְ לִשְׁחֹט עוֹד, הִלְכָּךְ אֵין זֶה טוֹעֶה אֶלָּא שׁוֹגֵג, דְּלֹא הָיָה לוֹ לִטְעוֹת בַּדָּבָר:


שיש להן קצבה – that he doesn’t see others engaged in its slaughtering, and since the daily whole offering was slaughtered, he knows that he does not have to slaughter another. Therefore, he does not err but rather makes an inadvertent mistake, for he didn’t have err in this matter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:12)


**תֹּאמַר בַּפֶסַח שֶׁאֵין לוֹ קִצְבָּה.** דְּהַכֹּל צְרִיכִין לְכָךְ, וַהֲרֵי הוּא רוֹאֶה אֲחֵרִים הַרְבֵּה עֲסוּקִים בְּכָךְ וְטָרוּד הוּא לְהִתְעַסֵּק בַּמִּצְוָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ שָׁחַט הוּא פֶסַח כְּבָר וּמָצָא זֶבַח זֶה עוֹמֵד בָּעֲזָרָה וּכְסָבוּר שֶׁהוּא פֶסַח וּשְׁחָטוֹ לְשֵׁם מִי שֶׁהוּא, טוֹעֶה בִדְבַר מִצְוָה הוּא:


תאמר בפסח שאין לו קצבה – for everyone needs this, and behold, he sees many others engaged in this and he is anxious to engage in the Mitzvah/commandment. For even if he had already slaughtered the Passover sacrifice and found this offering standing in the Temple courtyard, and he thought that it was the Passover offering and slaughtered it in the name of someone, he is in error about a matter of a Mitzvah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:5:13)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר.** הַשּׁוֹחֵט בְּשַׁבָּת זְבָחִים אֲחֵרִים כָּל יְמוֹת הַשָּׁנָה לְשֵׁם אֵמוּרֵי צִבּוּר פָּטוּר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


רבי מאיר אומר – He who slaughters on Shabbat other animal offerings (i.e., especially Festival offerings) all the days of the year for name of the sacrifices offered on the altar for the community, he is exempt. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua and the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:6:1)


**לְאוֹכְלָיו וְשֶׁלֹּא לְאוֹכְלָיו פָּטוּר וְכוּ'.** דְּהָא פֶּסַח כָּשֵׁר הוּא, דְּאָמְרִינַן פֶּרֶק תָּמִיד נִשְׁחָט דְּהַשּׁוֹחֵט לַפְּסוּלִים וְלַכְּשֵׁרִים לֹא פָסַל:


לאוכליו ושלא לאוכליו פטור וכו' – for this is a fit Passover offering as we said in the chapter “The whole burnt-offering that was slaughtered” (Chapter 5, Mishnah 3 of Tractate Pesahim), for a person who slaughters for the invalid and the valid is not invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:6:2)


**שְׁחָטוֹ וְנִמְצָא בַעַל מוּם חַיָּב.** דְּשׁוֹגֵג הוּא וְלֹא אָנוּס, שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ [לְבַקְּרוֹ]:


שחטו ונמצא בעל מום חייב – for it is an inadvertent error, and he was not forced into it, for he could have [checked/examined it].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:6:3)


**שֶׁמָּשְׁכוּ הַבְּעָלִים אֶת יָדָם.** מִמֶּנּוּ קֹדֶם שְׁחִיטָה וְנִמְנוּ עַל אַחֵר:


שמשכו בעלים את דים – [the owners withdrew their hands from it] before the slaughtering and registered on another


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:6:4)


**אוֹ שֶׁמֵּתוּ אוֹ שֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ.** דְּעַכְשָׁיו לֹא נִתְּנָה שַׁבָּת לִדָּחוֹת אֶצְלָם:


or that they died or that they were defiled, for now, they were not given Shabbat to override [the prohibitions]


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 6:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/6:6:5)


**פָּטוּר.** שֶׁאָנוּס הוּא, דְּלֹא הָיָה יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁכָּךְ הוּא וְלֹא הָיָה לוֹ לִבְדֹּק עַל כָּךְ:


to make them exempt for he was compelled, for he had not known that this was the situation and was not able to check/examine this.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:1:1)


**כֵּיצַד צוֹלִין.** שַׁפּוּד שֶׁל רִמּוֹן. וְלֹא שְׁאָר עֵצִים, דְּמַפְּקִי מַיָּא וַהֲוָה לֵיהּ בָּשֵׁל מְבֻשָּׁל בַּמַּיִם, וְרִמּוֹן לֹא מַפִּיק מַיָּא. וּבְשַׁפּוּד שֶׁל מַתֶּכֶת לֹא, כְּדִלְקַמָּן, דְּחַם מִקְצָתוֹ חַם כֻּלּוֹ וְנִצְלֶה מֵחֲמַת שַׁפּוּד, וְרַחֲמָנָא אָמַר צְלִי אֵשׁ וְלֹא צְלִי דָבָר אַחֵר:


כיצד צולין. שפוד של רומן – but not other woods that release water, for it must be (Exodus 12:9): “[Do not eat any of it raw,] or cooked in any way with water, [but roasted – head, legs and entrails -over the fire],” but pomegranate does not release water. But, not with a metal spit (Pesahim 74a), as earlier, when part of it warmed, all of it is warm and it is roasted from the heat of the spit, and the All-Merciful stated (Exodus 12:9), “roasted...over the fire,” and not roasted with something else.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:1:2)


**כְּמִין בִּשּׁוּל הוּא זֶה.** שֶׁמִּתְבַּשְּׁלִין בְּנֵי מֵעָיו לְתוֹכוֹ כִּבְתוֹךְ הַקְּדֵרָה:


כמין בשול הוא זה – when they cook the large intestines in it, like it is within the pot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:1:3)


**תּוֹלִין חוּצָה לוֹ.** תּוֹחֲבָן בַּשַּׁפּוּד לְמַעְלָה מִפִּיו שֶׁל טָלֶה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


תולין חוצה לו – they insert the spit above from the mouth of the lamb . And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:2:1)


**אָמַר רַבִּי צָדוֹק כוּ'.** מַתְנִיתִין חַסּוֹרֵי מִחַסְּרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, וְאִם אַסְכָּלָה מְנֻקֶּבֶת הִיא כְּשֵׁרָה, וְאָמַר רַבִּי צָדוֹק וְכוּ', וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי צָדוֹק:


אמר רבי צדוק וכו' – Our Mishnah is deficient and should be read as follows: If the grille is perforated, it is fit. And Rabbi Tzadok said, etc. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Tzadok.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:2:2)


**נָגַע הַפֶּסַח בְּחַרְסוֹ שֶׁל תַּנּוּר יִקְלֹּף.** מִן הַפֶּסַח מָקוֹם הַנּוֹגֵעַ בַּתַּנּוּר, שֶׁנִּצְלָה שָׁם מֵחֹם חֶרֶס הַתַּנּוּר וְלֹא צְלִי אֵשׁ הוּא:


נגע הפסח בחרסו של תנור יקלף – [he should peel off] from the Passover [sacrifice] at the place where it touches the oven, for it roasted there from the heat of the earthenware of the oven and it is not roasted by fire [as required by Biblical law -see Exodus 12:9].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:2:3)


**נָטַף מֵרָטְבּוֹ עַל הַחֶרֶס וְחָזַר אֵלָיו.** שֶׁחָזַר שֻׁמָּן שֶׁנִּתְחַמֵּם מִן הַחֶרֶס וְנִבְלַע בַּפֶּסַח:


נטף מרוטבו על החרס וחזר אליו – that the fat that had been warmed by the earthenware went back and was absorbed by the Passover [offering].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:2:4)


**יִטֹּל אֶת מְקוֹמוֹ.** וְלֹא סַגִּי בִקְלִיפָה אֶלָּא בִנְטִילָה כָּעֳבִי אֶצְבַּע, דְּשֻׁמָּן נִבְלָע בְּתוֹכוֹ הַרְבֵּה, וְאוֹתוֹ שֻׁמָּן צָלִי מִן הַחֶרֶס הוּא:


יטול את מקומו – and peeling is insufficient other than by taking the like the thickness of a finger’s worth. For the fat was absorbed in it a great deal and that fat is roasted from the earthenware.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:2:5)


**נָטַף מֵרָטְבּוֹ עַל הַסֹּלֶת.** וְהַסֹּלֶת רוֹתַחַת, דְּמִצְלֵּי רֹטֶב מֵחֲמַת סֹלֶת, וְהַהוּא רֹטֶב שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַסֹּלֶת אָסוּר לְמֵיכְלֵיהּ, דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ צָלִי מֵחֲמַת דָּבָר אַחֵר, הִלְכָּךְ יִקְמֹץ אֶת מְקוֹמוֹ וְיִשְׂרֹף אוֹתוֹ קֹמֶץ כִּשְׁאָר קָדָשִׁים הַפְּסוּלִים:


נטף מרוטבו על הסולת – and the flour is heated (Pesahim 76a) which roasts the gravy on account of the flour, and that gravy that is within the flour is forbidden to eat since it was roasted on account of something else. Therefore, he takes a handful from its place and burns that handful like other impure Holy Things.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:3:1)


**סָכוֹ בְשֶׁמֶן תְּרוּמָה.** שֶׁמֻּתָּר לָסוּךְ הַפֶּסַח בְּמֵי פֵרוֹת:


סכו בשמן תרומה – since it is permitted to based the Passover [offering] with fruit juice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:3:2)


**אִם חַי יְדִיחֶנּוּ.** דְּלֹא בָלַע:


אם חי ידיחנו – that it did not absorb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:3:3)


**וְאִם צָלִי הוּא.** בָּעֵי קְלִיפָה מִשּׁוּם דְּבָלַע:


ואם צלי הוא – it requires peeling because it absorbed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:3:4)


**שֶׁאֵין פּוֹדִים.** מוֹכְרִים:


שאין פודין – sell


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:3:5)


**מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי בִירוּשָׁלַיִם.** וַאֲפִלּוּ לְאָכְלוֹ בְטָהֳרָה, דִּכְתִיב (דברים יד) וְנָתַתָּ בַּכָּסֶף וְצַרְתָּ הַכֶּסֶף בְּיָדְךָ וְהָלַכְתָּ אֶל הַמָּקוֹם:


מעשר שני בירושלים – and even to eat it in ritual purity, as it is written (Deuteronomy 14:25): “You may convert them into money. Wrap up the money and take it with you to the place [that the LORD your God has chosen].”



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:4:1)


**חֲמִשָּׁה דְבָרִים.** אֵין לְךָ בְצִבּוּר קָרְבָּן נֶאֱכָל אֶלָּא אֵלּוּ, וְקָרְבַּן צִבּוּר דּוֹחֶה אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה, דְּיָלְפִינַן מִפֶּסַח דִּכְתִיב בֵּיהּ בְּמוֹעֲדוֹ, וְדוֹחֶה אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה כְּשֶׁרֹב הַקָּהָל טְמֵאִים, דְּהָכִי אָמְרִינַן אִישׁ כִּי יִהְיֶה טָמֵא וְכוּ', יָחִיד נִדְחֶה וְאֵין צִבּוּר נִדְחִין, וּבְקָרְבְּנוֹת צִבּוּר כְּתִיב (במדבר כט) אֵלֶּה תַּעֲשׂוּ לַה'‏ בְּמוֹעֲדֵיכֶם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בַּפֶּסַח בְּמוֹעֲדוֹ, וְאַשְׁמְעִינַן מַתְנִיתִין דְּאַף עַל גַּב דִּקְרֵבִין בְּטֻמְאָה לְאַפּוֹקֵי צִבּוּר יְדֵי חוֹבָתָן, אֵין נֶאֱכָלִים בְּטֻמְאָה. וְהָא דְתָנֵי חֲמִשָּׁה דְבָרִים וְלֹא תָנֵי קָרְבְּנוֹת צִבּוּר וְכוּ', מִנְיָנָא לְמַעוֹטֵי חֲגִיגַת הָרְגָלִים כֻּלָּם, דְּדָמוּ לְקָרְבַּן צִבּוּר מִשּׁוּם דְּאָתוּ בִכְנוּפְיָא בַּאֲסֵפַת חֲבֵרִים לָרֶגֶל, וְאֵינָם דּוֹחִים אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁאֵין דּוֹחִים אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת:


חמשה דברים – there is no community sacrifice that is eaten other than these, and the community sacrifice overrides the impurity, as we derive from the Passover [sacrifice] as it is written regarding it (Numbers 9:2): “[Let the Israelite people offer the Passover sacrifice] at its set time,” and overrides the defilement when most of the community are impure, for such we say (Numbers 9:10): “When any of you or of your posterity who are defiled….” The individual is postponed, but the community is not postponed. And regarding community sacrifices, it is written (Numbers 29:29): “All these shall you offer [to the LORD] at the stated times,” so just as it is stated with regard to Passover, “at its set time,” and our Mishnah teaches us that even though they are offered [in a state of] impurity, to fulfill the community’s religious obligation, they are not eaten in impurity. And that we are taught five things and are not taught community sacrifices, etc., their number excludes the Festival offerings of the Festivals that are compared to the individual sacrifice because they come at the gathering of the assembly of members for the festivals and do not override the impurity in the same manner that they do not override the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:4:2)


**זִבְחֵי שַׁלְמֵי צִבּוּר.** כִּבְשֵׂי עֲצֶרֶת, שֶׁאֵין שְׁלָמִים אֲחֵרִים לַצִּבּוּר:


זבחי שלמי צבור – the lambs of Atzeret/Shavuot, for there are no other peace-offerings for the community.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:4:3)


**וּשְׂעִירֵי רָאשֵׁי חֳדָשִׁים.** וְלֹא תָנֵי שְׂעִירֵי רְגָלִים, דְּכֵיוָן דְּאַשְׁמְעִינַן זִבְחֵי שַׁלְמֵי צִבּוּר שֶׁהֵן מִינֵי דָמִים וְאֵין נֶאֱכָלִין בְּטֻמְאָה, הוּא הַדִּין לְכָל שְׁאָר מִינֵי דָמִים, וְלֹא אִיצְטְרִיךְ לְמִתְנֵי שְׂעִירֵי רָאשֵׁי חֳדָשִׁים אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם דְּלָא כְתִיב בְּהוּ מוֹעֵד, וְסָלְקָא דַעְתָּךְ אֲמֵינָא לֹא לִדְחוּ אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה, דְּהָא כָּל קָרְבְּנוֹת צִבּוּר מִמּוֹעֵד נָפְקָא לָן, דִּכְתִיב בְּכֻלְּהוּ אֵלֶּה תַּעֲשׂוּ לַה'‏ בְּמוֹעֲדֵיכֶם:


ושעירי ראשי חדשים – and it did not teach the goats of the festival, for since we have learned that the community peace-offering sacrifices are kinds of life and they are not eaten in impurity and the same law applies for all the rest of the kinds of life, and the Mishnah did not need to teach the goats of the New Moons except for the fact that it is not written regarding them, “its set time”/”Moed” and you might think that they do not override the defilement for all the community sacrifices are derived from “Moed”/set time/Festive season, as it is written regarding all of them (Numbers 29:29): “All these shall you offer [to the LORD] at the stated times.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:4:4)


**שֶׁלֹּא בָא מִתְּחִלָּתוֹ אֶלָּא לַאֲכִילָה.** שֶׁכְּשֶׁנִּצְטַוָּה עִקַּר מִצְוַת פֶּסַח לַאֲכִילָה נִצְטַוָּה, דִּכְתִיב (שמות יב) לְפִי אָכְלוֹ, וְכִי שַׁרְיֵהּ רַחֲמָנָא לַאֲתוֹיֵי בְטֻמְאָה מֵאִישׁ נִדְחֶה וְאֵין צִבּוּר נִדְחִין, אַדַּעְתָּא לְמֵיכְלֵיהּ שַׁרְיֵהּ:


שלא בא מתחלתו אלא לאכילה – for when the essential commandment of the Passover [sacrifice] was commanded, it was ordained for eating, as it is written (Exodus 12:4): “…according to what each household will eat,” and when the All-Merciful permitted to include that defilement from a person is postponed, but a community is not postponed, with the knowledge that eating is permitted.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:5:1)


**נִטְמָא בָשָׂר וְהַחֵלֶב קַיָּם אֵינוֹ זוֹרֵק אֶת הַדָּם.** דְּעִקַּר פֶּסַח לַאֲכִילַת אָדָם אָתֵי:


**The meat became impure but the forbidden fats remains [pure], one may not sprinkle [its] blood [on the alter]** since the reason for the coming of the paschal sacrifice is for eating.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:5:2)


**וּבַמֻּקְדָּשִׁים אֵינוֹ כֵן.** שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ נִטְמָא בָשָׂר וְחֵלֶב קַיָּם, זוֹרֵק אֶת הַדָּם:


**And it is not like this with [other] offerings** that even if the meat becomes impure and the forbidden fats remain [pure], [its] blood is sprinkled.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:6:1)


**נִטְמָא קָהָל.** בְּטֻמְאַת מֵת, שֶׁאֵין פֶּסַח נִדְחֶה מִפְּנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה אֶלָּא מִטֻּמְאַת מֵת:


נטמא הקהל – through defilement with the dead, for the Passover [sacrifice] is not postponed because of the defilement other than from defilement with the dead (Zevahim 22b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:6:2)


**יַעֲשׂוּ בְטֻמְאָה.** וַאֲפִלּוּ טְמֵאִים יְחִידִים, דְּהָא [פֶסַח] דִּטְהוֹרִים גּוּפַיְהוּ בְּטֻמְאָה אָתוּ מִשּׁוּם כֹּהֲנִים, שֶׁאֵין קָרְבַּן צִבּוּר הַבָּא בְטֻמְאָה חָלוּק, מֵאַחַר שֶׁקָּרְבַּן רֻבָּם בָּא בְטֻמְאָה אַף הַיְחִידִים עוֹשִׂים בְּטֻמְאָה:


יעשו בטומאה – and even impure individuals for [the Passover (Sacrifice)] of pure individuals which itself is offered in impurity comes because of the Kohanim, for the community sacrifice that comes in defilement is not divided/interrupted, for after a sacrifice of the majority comes in impurity, even the individuals do them in impurity.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:1)


**וְאַחַר כָּךְ נוֹדַע שֶׁהוּא טָמֵא.** הַפֶּסַח אוֹ הַדָּם:


ואח"כ נודע שהוא טמא – The Passover [offering] or the blood [became defiled].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:2)


**הַצִּיץ מְרַצֶּה.** וּפָטוּר מִפֶּסַח שֵׁנִי:


הציץ – effects pardon and he is exempt from the Second Passover.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:3)


**נִטְמָא.** בְּעָלָיו טֻמְאַת הַגּוּף בְּמֵת:


נטמא – its owner the defilement of the body with the dead.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:4)


**אֵין הַצִּיץ מְרַצֶּה.** וְחַיָּב בְּפֶסַח שֵׁנִי, דְּהָא בִּשְׁעַת זְרִיקָה לָאו בַּר מֶעְבָּד פֶּסַח הוּא, דְּרַחֲמָנָא דַחְיֵהּ:


אין הציץ מרצה – and he is obligated for the Second Passover, for at the time of the sprinkling [of the blood] it is not someone making the Passover [offering] for the All-Merciful has postponed it..


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:5)


**הַנָּזִיר.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ו) וְכִי יָמוּת מֵת עָלָיו [וְגוֹ'] וְהַיָּמִים הָרִאשׁוֹנִים יִפְּלוּ, דְּאִם נִטְמָא בְמֵת קֹדֶם הֲבָאַת קָרְבְּנוֹתָיו סוֹתֵר:


הנזיר – as it is written (Numbers 6:7): “If a person dies [suddenly] near him, [defiling his sacred hair]…,”(Numbers 6:12): “the previous period shall be void, [since his consecrated hair was defiled],” for if he had been defiled by [contact with] the dead before the bringing of his sacrifices, it makes void the days of a Nazirite’s vow which have been observed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:6)


**הַצִּיץ מְרַצֶּה עַל טֻמְאַת הַדָּם.** וְתִגְלַחְתּוֹ כְשֵׁרָה וּמֻתָּר לִשְׁתּוֹת יַיִן וְלִטָּמֵא לַמֵּתִים:


הציץ מרצה על טומאת הדם – and his shaving is valid and he is permitted to drink wine and to become defiled to dead people.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:7)


**וְאֵין הַצִּיץ מְרַצֶּה עַל טֻמְאַת הַגּוּף.** וְאִם [טָמֵא] בִּשְׁעַת הֲבָאַת קָרְבְּנוֹתָיו סוֹתֵר הַכֹּל:


ואין הציץ מרצה על טומאת הגוף – any defilement by the dead that a person did not know of prior to this is called טומאת התהום /a grave in the depth (i.e., covered up uncleanness discovered).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:8)


**נִטְמָא.** גּוּפוֹ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:9)


**בְּטֻמְאַת הַתְּהוֹם.** כָּל טֻמְאַת מֵת שֶׁלֹּא יָדַע בָּהּ אָדָם קֹדֶם לָכֵן נִקְרֵאת טֻמְאַת הַתְּהוֹם:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:7:10)


**הַצִּיץ מְרַצֶּה.** דַּהֲלָכָה לְמֹשֶׁה מִסִּינַי שֶׁהֻתַּר טֻמְאַת הַתְּהוֹם לָהֶם, כֵּיוָן שֶׁלֹּא הִכִּיר בָּהּ אָדָם בְּטֻמְאָה זוֹ מֵעוֹלָם. וְאִם נוֹדַע לוֹ בְטֻמְאָה זוֹ אַחַר שֶׁהִקְרִיב פִּסְחוֹ אֵין צָרִיךְ לַעֲשׂוֹת פֶּסַח שֵׁנִי. וְכֵן נָזִיר שֶׁנּוֹדַע לוֹ בְטֻמְאַת הַתְּהוֹם לְאַחַר שֶׁהֵבִיא קָרְבְּנוֹתָיו אֵין צָרִיךְ לְהָבִיא קָרְבַּן טֻמְאָה. וְלֹא אָמְרוּ טֻמְאַת הַתְּהוֹם אֶלָּא בְּטֻמְאַת מֵת בִּלְבַד:


הציץ מרצה – It is a usage dating from Moses as delivered at Sinai (i.e., a traditional interpretation of a written law) that the grave in the depth that was permitted to them, since he did not recognize a person with this defilement ever, and if it this defilement became known to him after he had offered his Passover [sacrifice], he doesn’t need to do the Second Passover. And similarly, a Nazirite to whom it became known about a grave in the depth (i.e., covered up uncleanness discovered), after he brought his sacrifices, he does not need to bring a sacrifice of defilement, for they did not mention the grave in the depth other than with defilement from [contact with] the dead alone.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:8:1)


**לִפְנֵי הַבִּירָה.** כָּל הַמִּקְדָּשׁ כֻּלּוֹ קָרוּי בִּירָה. וְשׂוֹרְפִים אוֹתוֹ שָׁם כְּדֵי לַבַיְּשָׁן, שֶׁיִּזָּהֲרוּ שֶׁלֹּא יָבֹאוּ עוֹד לִידֵי טֻמְאָה:


לפני הבירה – The entirety of the Temple is called בירה/the chosen, Divine residence. So that they would be careful that they are not in contact with defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:8:2)


**מֵעֲצֵי הַמַּעֲרָכָה.** שֶׁלֹּא לְבַיֵּשׁ אֶת מִי שֶׁאֵין לוֹ עֵצִים:


מעצי המערכה – so that they do not embarrass whomever who lacks wood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:8:3)


**נִטְמָא מִעוּטוֹ.** וְכֵן נוֹתָר שֶׁל פֶּסַח טָהוֹר:


מיעוטו – and similarly, portions of the sacrifices left over beyond the legal time and bound to be burned of the pure Passover [offering].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:8:4)


**הַצַּיְקָנִין.** הָעַצְרָנִין. צָרֵי עַיִן, שׂוֹרְפִין אוֹתוֹ מִעוּט שֶׁנִּטְמָא וְהַנּוֹתָר מִפֶּסַח טָהוֹר, לִפְנֵי הַבִּירָה:


הציקנין – (Pesahim 82a) – the miserly. Narrow-minded/selfish, burn that lesser part that had been defiled and the remnant portions of the sacrifices left over beyond the legal time and bound to be burned from a pure Passover [sacrifice], in front of the chosen, Divine residence.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:9:1)


**הַפֶּסַח שֶׁיָּצָא.** חוּץ לַחוֹמָה:


הפסח שיצא – outside of the walls [of Jerusalem].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:9:2)


**יִשָּׂרֵף מִיָּד.** בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר, וְלֹא צָרִיךְ לְאַשְׁהוֹיֵי עַד הַבֹּקֶר שֶׁל חֲמִשָּׁה עָשָׂר כְּדֵי שֶׁתְּעֻבַּר צוּרָתוֹ דְּהַיְנוּ שֶׁיָּבֹא לִידֵי נוֹתָר. וּמִיהוּ בְּיוֹם טוֹב לֹא מָצֵי שָׂרֵיף לֵיהּ, דְּאֵין שׂוֹרְפִין קָדָשִׁים בְּיוֹם טוֹב:


ישרף מיד – [it should be burned immediately] on the fourteenth [of Nisan] and he does not have to delay until the morning of the fifteenth in order that it is appearance is allowed to spoil (i.e., wait until the flesh looks disfigured by beginning decay), that is, that it should come to be portions of the sacrifices left over beyond the legal time and bound to be burned. But however, on the Holiday/Yom Tov, he is not able to burn it as we don’t burn Holy Things on Yom Tov.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:9:3)


**נִטְמְאוּ בְעָלִים אוֹ שֶׁמֵּתוּ.** אֵין פְּסוּלוֹ בְגוּפוֹ אֶלָּא מֵחֲמַת דָּבָר אַחֵר:


נטמאו בעלים או שמתו – there is no invalidation of his body other than on account of something else.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:9:4)


**תְּעֻבַּר צוּרָתוֹ.** יַמְתִּין עַד שֶׁיָּבֹא לִידֵי נוֹתָר, וְאַחַר כָּךְ יִשְׂרְפֶנּוּ:


תעובר צורתו – He should wait until it comes to be portions left over beyond the legal time, and afterwards he should burn it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:9:5)


**אַף זֶה יִשָּׂרֵף מִיָּד.** רַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן בְּרוֹקָא לֹא פָלֵיג אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא אֶלָּא כְּשֶׁנִּטְמְאוּ הַבְּעָלִים אוֹ מֵתוּ קֹדֶם זְרִיקַת הַדָּם, דְּלֹא אִיחֲזֵי בָּשָׂר בַּאֲכִילָה וַהֲוָה לֵיהּ כִּפְסוּלוֹ בְגוּפוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן בְּרוֹקָא:


אף זה ישרף מיד – Rabbi Yohanan ben Beroka does not disagree with the First Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] other than when the owners became defiled or they had died prior to the tossing of the blood, and it is not considered meat to be eaten and it is for him like an invalidation of his body. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yohanan ben Beroka.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:10:1)


**הָעֲצָמוֹת.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְשָׁבְרָן, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנֶּאֶסְרָה שְׁבִירַת עֶצֶם, וְנוֹתָר בָּהֶן מֹחַ וְטָעוּן שְׂרֵפָה:


העצמות – for he is not able to break them because the break of the bone is prohibited, and there remains among them the brain/marrow which requires burning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:10:2)


**וְהַגִּידִין.** שֶׁהֵן אֲסוּרִין בַּאֲכִילָה וְאֵין חַיָּבִין עֲלֵיהֶן מִן הַתּוֹרָה, כְּגוֹן שֻׁמָּנוֹ שֶׁל גִּיד הַנָּשֶׁה, שֶׁהוּא מֻתָּר, אֶלָּא שֶׁיִּשְׂרָאֵל קְדוֹשִׁים נָהֲגוּ בוֹ אִסּוּר. אִי נַמִּי הַגִּיד הַחִיצוֹן הַסָּמוּךְ לַבָּשָׂר, שֶׁהוּא אָסוּר, וְאֵין חַיָּבִין עָלָיו, וּמִן הַתּוֹרָה הָוֵי נוֹתָר, וּלְאָכְלוֹ אִי אֶפְשָׁר דְּרַבָּנָן אֲסָרוּהוּ:


והגידין – which are forbidden to be eaten and one is not liable for them from the Torah, such as the case of the fat of the sciatic nerve which is permissible, but holy Jews practiced regarding them with a prohibition. Alternatively, the outer sinew which is closest to the skin which is prohibited, and we are not liable concerning it, but from the Torah, it is a remnant left over beyond the legal time, and it is impossible to eat it for the Rabbis prohibited it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:10:3)


**וְהַנּוֹתָר.** בְּשַׂר פֶּסַח שֶׁנִּתְעַצְּלוּ בַאֲכִילָתוֹ וְנִתּוֹתַר:


והנותר – the meat of the Passover [offering] that they had been careless about while eating it and it became a remnant left over beyond the legal time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:10:4)


**בְּשִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר.** שֶׁהוּא חֻלּוֹ שֶׁל מוֹעֵד. אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּפְסַל בְּבֹקֶר רִאשׁוֹן אֵין רַשַּׁאי לְשָׂרְפוֹ בְּיוֹם טוֹב, דִּשְׂרֵפַת קָדָשִׁים עֲשֵׂה, וְיוֹם טוֹב עֲשֵׂה וְלֹא תַעֲשֶׂה, וְאֵין עֲשֵׂה דוֹחֶה אֶת לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה וַעֲשֵׂה:


ששה עשר – which is the Intermediate Days of the Festival. Even though it had become defiled in the morning of the first [day], it is not permitted to burn it on the Holy Day, for the burning of Holy Things is a positive commandment, and the Holy Day is a positive commandment and a negative commandment, and a positive commandment does not override a negative commandment and a positive commandment.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:11:1)


**כָּל הַנֶּאֱכָל בְּשׁוֹר הַגָּדוֹל.** שֶׁכְּבָר הֻקְשָׁה כָּל הֶעָתִיד לְהִקָּשׁוֹת בּוֹ:


כל הנאכל בשור הגדול – that already had become distasteful, all in the future will get harder.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:11:2)


**יֵאָכֵל בִּגְדִי הָרַךְ.** רָאוּי לַאֲכִילָה בְּפֶסַח בֶּן שְׁמֹנָה יָמִים. אֲבָל מַה שֶּׁאֵין נֶאֱכָל בְּשׁוֹר הַגָּדוֹל אֵין נִמְנִין עָלָיו בְּפֶסַח אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעַכְשָׁיו הוּא רַךְ, הוֹאִיל וְסוֹפוֹ לְהִקָּשׁוֹת:


יאכל בגדי הרך – appropriate for eating on Passover all eight days. But what is not eaten with a large ox, is not registered upon it during Passover, even though that it is soft now, since it will get hard over the course of time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:11:3)


**וְרָאשֵׁי כְנָפַיִם וְהַסְּחוּסִים.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, וּמַהוּ הַנֶּאֱכָל בְּשׁוֹר הַגָּדוֹל, רָאשֵׁי כְנָפַיִם, הַסְּחוּס שֶׁקּוֹרִין טנרו"ס בְּלַעַ"ז שֶׁבְּרֹאשׁ כַּף הַכָּתֵף, וּשְׁאָר סְחוּסִים כְּגוֹן תְּנוּךְ הָאֹזֶן וּסְחוּסֵי הֶחָזֶה וְהַצְּלָעוֹת הַקְּטַנּוֹת שֶׁבְּסוֹף הַשִּׁדְרָה, הוֹאִיל וּבְשׁוֹר הַגָּדוֹל נֶאֱכָלִים עַל יְדֵי שְׁלִיקָה, יֵאָכְלוּ בִגְדִי הָרַךְ צָלִי:


וראשי כנפים וסחוסים – This is how it should be read: And that which is eaten of the big ox – the ends of the shoulder blades, the gristles, which we call טנרוס /TANROS in the foreign language that are at the head of the shoulder blade and the rest of the gristles such as the ear-lobe and the gristles of the breast and the small ribs at the end of the spine, since the big ox which is eaten by boiling, they eat the soft lamb roasted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:11:4)


**אֲבָל הַמּוֹתִיר בַּטָּהוֹר כוּ'.** דִּכְתִיב (שמות יב) וְלֹא תוֹתִירוּ מִמֶּנּוּ עַד בֹּקֶר וְהַנֹּתָר מִמֶּנּוּ עַד בֹּקֶר בָּאֵשׁ תִּשְׂרֹפוּ, בָּא הַכָּתוּב לִתֵּן עֲשֵׂה אַחַר לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה, לוֹמַר אִם עָבַרְתָּ עַל לָאו, קַיֵּם עֲשֵׂה שֶׁבּוֹ וְאֵינְךָ לוֹקֶה. אִי נַמִּי דַּהֲוֵי לָאו שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ מַעֲשֶׂה, וְכָל לָאו שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ מַעֲשֶׂה אֵין לוֹקִין עָלָיו:


אבל המותיר בטהור וכו' – as it is written (Exodus 12:10): “You may not leave any of it over until morning; if any of it is left until morning, you shall burn it.” The Biblical verses comes to give a positive commandment after a negative commandment, to say that if you transgressed the negative commandment, fulfill the positive command and you won’t be flogged. Alternatively, since it is a negative commandment which has no action with it, and any negative commandment that doesn’t have an action associated with it, one is not flogged for it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:11:5)


**וְהַשּׁוֹבֵר בַּטָּמֵא כוּ'.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְעֶצֶם לֹא תִשְׁבְּרוּ בוֹ, בַּכָּשֵׁר מֻזְהָר עַל שְׁבִירַת עֶצֶם, וְאֵינוֹ מֻזְהָר עַל שְׁבִירַת עֶצֶם בַּפָּסוּל:


והשובר בטמא וכו' – as it is written (Exodus 12:46): “nor shall you break a bone of it,” a kosher/fit individual is warned on the breaking of a bone, but he is not warned on the breaking of a bone of something invalid.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:1)


**אֵבָר.** מִן הַפֶּסַח:


**A limb** from the Paschal sacrifice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:2)


**שֶׁיָּצָא מִקְצָתוֹ.** חוּץ לִמְחִצָּתוֹ, וְצָרִיךְ לַחְתֹּךְ אֶת הַיּוֹצֵא דִּכְתִיב (שמות כב) וּבָשָׂר בַּשָּׂדֶה טְרֵפָה לֹא תֹאכֵלוּ, וְדָרְשִׁינַן בֵּיהּ כָּל בָּשָׂר שֶׁיָּצָא בַשָּׂדֶה כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁיָּצָא חוּץ לִמְחִצָּתוֹ, כְּגוֹן קָדְשֵׁי קָדָשִׁים שֶׁיָּצְאוּ חוּץ לָעֲזָרָה וְקָדָשִׁים קַלִּים שֶׁיָּצְאוּ חוּץ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם, אוֹתוֹ בָשָׂר הַיּוֹצֵא טְרֵפָה הוּא וְלֹא תֹאכֵלוּ:


**Part of [a limb] left** [the group's] boundaries, and it is necessary to chop the portion that exited; as it says (Exodus 22) "And meat that was torn (died without slaughter) in the field you should not eat," this is explained to refer to any flesh that was left out in the field i.e. it left it's boundaries; Like holy of holies that went beyond the courtyard or lesser holies that exited Jerusalem: that meat that left is considered "torn" and you should not eat it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:3)


**חוֹתֵךְ.** אֶת הַבָּשָׂר הַיּוֹצֵא סָבִיב עַד שֶׁמַּגִּיעַ לָעֶצֶם:


**Cut** the part of the meat that exited in a circle until the bone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:4)


**וְקוֹלֵף.** אֶת הַבָּשָׂר שֶׁלֹּא יָצָא לַחוּץ עַד שֶׁמַּגִּיעַ לַפֶּרֶק שֶׁמִּתְחַבְּרִים שָׁם שְׁנֵי עֲצָמוֹת, וְאוֹכֵל אֶת הַבָּשָׂר שֶׁקָּלַף, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא יָצָא, וְחוֹתֵךְ אֶת הַפֶּרֶק וּמַשְׁלִיךְ כָּל הָעֶצֶם שֶׁיָּצָא מִקְצָתוֹ:


**And peel** the meat that didn't depart until it reaches the joint, and eat the peeled meat since it did not exit, and chop the joint and send off the entire bone of which part left the boundary.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:5)


**וּבַמֻּקְדָּשִׁים.** בִּשְׁאָר קָדָשִׁים חוּץ מֵהַפֶּסַח:


**And with mukdashim:** other sacrifices sans the paschal sacrifice cleave the portion that exited (through the bone) with a cleaver. With the paschal sacrifice it is forbidden to do this, since it is prohibited from having a bone broken.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:6)


**קוֹצֵץ בַּקּוֹפִיץ.** אוֹתוֹ מִקְצָת הָאֵבָר שֶׁיָּצָא. וּבַפֶּסַח אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לַעֲשׂוֹת כֵּן, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא מֻזְהָר עַל שְׁבִירַת עֶצֶם:


**He cut it with a hatchet:** The same part of the limb that came out. And on Passover, he cannot do so because he is warned about breaking a bone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:7)


**מִן הָאֲגַף וְלִפְנִים כְּלִפְנִים.** אֲגַף קְרִי כָּל מָקוֹם הֲגָפַת הַדְּלָתוֹת שֶׁהוּא חוֹפֵף וְנוֹקֵשׁ שָׁם כְּשֶׁסּוֹגְרוֹ, דְּהַיְנוּ [מִשְּׂפַת הַפְּנִימִי שֶׁל עֳבִי הַפֶּתַח עַד מְקוֹם הַנְּקִישָׁה. מִן הָאֲגַף וְלִפְנִים, דְּהַיְנוּ] תּוֹךְ הָעִיר מַמָּשׁ:


**From the door-wing and inside:** A door-wing is any place where the door closes i.e. the place that the door overlaps and taps when it is closed. (This is the inner lip of thick portion of the door until the spot of closure. From the wing inwards.) This is entirely inside the city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:8)


**כְּלִפְנִים.** וְאוֹכְלִים שָׁם קָדָשִׁים קַלִּים:


**As inside:** and eat there the lesser holies.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:9)


**מִן הָאֲגַף וְלַחוּץ.** דְּהַיְנוּ מִמְּקוֹם הַנְּקִישָׁה וְלַחוּץ, כְּלַחוּץ:


**From the door-wing and towards the outside**: This is from the point of closure outwards, it is considered outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:12:10)


**הַחַלּוֹנוֹת.** שֶׁבְּחוֹמַת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם, וְעֹבִי רֹאשׁ הַחוֹמָה בְגַגָּהּ, כְּלִפְנִים:


**The windows** that are in the walls of Jerusalem. And the thickness of the top of the wall in the roof, is considered like the inside.



### Mishnah 13

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:13:1)


**שְׁתֵּי חֲבוּרוֹת שֶׁהָיוּ אוֹכְלוֹת.** פֶּסַח אֶחָד:


שתי חבורות שהיו אוכלות – one Passover [offering].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:13:2)


**אֵלּוּ הוֹפְכִים פְּנֵיהֶם הֵילָךְ וְאֵלּוּ הוֹפְכִים פְּנֵיהֶם הֵילָךְ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ נִרְאִים כִּשְׁתֵּי חֲבוּרוֹת עַל פֶּסַח אֶחָד לֹא אִכְפַּת לָן, דְּרַחֲמָנָא אָמַר (שם יב) עַל הַבָּתִּים אֲשֶׁר יֹאכְלוּ אֹתוֹ, דְּמַשְׁמַע שְׁנֵי בְנֵי אָדָם אוֹכְלִין פֶּסַח אֶחָד בִּשְׁנֵי בָּתִּים, דִּכְתִיב יֹאכְלוּ [שְׁנַיִם], אוֹתוֹ חַד פֶּסַח, [בָּתִּים] שְׁנַיִם. אֲבָל אָדָם אֶחָד אֵינוֹ אוֹכֵל בִּשְׁנֵי מְקוֹמוֹת, וּלְהָכִי מוֹקֵי בְּבַיִת אֶחָד יֵאָכֵל, שֶׁאֵין אֶחָד אוֹכֵל בִּשְׁנֵי בָּתִּים. וּשְׁתֵּי חֲבוּרוֹת הַהוֹפְכוֹת פְּנֵיהֶם כִּשְׁנֵי בָתִּים דָּמֵי:


אלו הופכים פניהם הילך ואלו הופכים פניהם הילך – and even if they appear as two groups eating one Passover [sacrifice], we don’t care, for the All-Merciful said (Exodus 12:7): “of the houses in which they are to eat it,” which implies two people eating one Passover [sacrifice] in two houses, as it is written: "יאכלו" /”they are to eat it” [two] "אתו"/”it” - one Passover [sacrifice] in two [houses]. And both groups turn their faces like they are in two homes. (see Talmud Pesahim 86a)


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:13:3)


**וְהַמֵּחַם.** שֶׁמְּחַמְּמִין בּוֹ אֶת הַחַמִּין שֶׁמּוֹזְגִים בּוֹ הַיַּיִן, רַשַּׁאי לִתֵּן בָּאֶמְצַע כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא נוֹחַ לִשְׁתֵּיהֶן לִמְזֹג לְכָאן וּלְכָאן. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמַּפְסִיק בֵּין הַחֲבוּרוֹת, לֹא אִכְפַּת לָן:


והמיחם – that warms up the warm foods that they pour in it wine, it is permissible to place it in the middle in order that it be pleasing for both groups to pour to here and to there, and even though that it separates between the groups, we don’t care.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:13:4)


**וּכְשֶׁהַשַּׁמָּשׁ.** הַמְשַׁמֵּשׁ לִשְׁתֵּיהֶן, עוֹמֵד מֵחֲבוּרָה אַחַת שֶׁהִתְחִיל לֶאֱכֹל הַפֶּסַח עִמָּהּ וּמוֹזֵג לַחֲבוּרָה הָאַחֶרֶת:


וכשהשמש – [the waiter] who serves both group, stands from one group – one when he began to eat the Passover [sacrifice] with it (i.e., the group) and pours for the other group.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:13:5)


**קוֹפֵץ אֶת פִּיו.** סוֹגֵר וּבוֹלֵם אֶת פִּיו וּמַחְזִיר אֶת פָּנָיו לְצַד חֲבוּרָתוֹ, שֶׁלֹּא יַחְשְׁדוּהוּ שֶׁהוּא אוֹכֵל עִם הַחֲבוּרָה הָאַחֶרֶת, דְּאֵין אָדָם אֶחָד אוֹכֵל בִּשְׁתֵּי חֲבוּרוֹת כִּדְאָמְרָן:


קופץ את פיו – he closes and muzzles his mouth and turns his face to the side of his group, that they should not suspect him that he is eating with the other group, for one person cannot eat with two groups, as we have said.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 7:13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/7:13:6)


**וְהַכַּלָּה.** שֶׁהִיא בוֹשָׁה, רַשָּׁאָה לַהֲפֹךְ פָּנֶיהָ לְצַד אַחֵר וְלֶאֱכֹל, דְּפֶסַח נֶאֱכָל בִּשְׁתֵּי חֲבוּרוֹת:


והכלה – who is embarrassed, is permitted to turn her face to the other side and to eat, as the Passover [offering] is eaten in two groups.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:1:1)


**הָאִשָּׁה.** תֹּאכַל מִשֶּׁל בַּעְלָהּ. כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא פֵרְשָׁה שֶׁדַּעְתָּהּ בְּשֶׁל אָבִיהָ, דְּמִסְּתָמָא דַּעְתָּהּ לִמָּנוֹת עַל שֶׁל בַּעְלָהּ:


האשה תאכל משל בעלה – all the time that she did not explain that her mind/intentions are with her father, for generally her intention is to be counted with her husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:1:2)


**רֶגֶל הָרִאשׁוֹן.** שֶׁלְּאַחַר נִשּׂוּאֶיהָ, שֶׁכָּךְ דֶּרֶךְ הַנְּשׂוּאוֹת לָלֶכֶת לְבֵית אֲבִיהֶן:


רגל הראשון – that was after her marriage, for it is the manner of married women to go to the house of their fathers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:1:3)


**תֹּאכַל בִּמְקוֹם שֶׁהִיא רוֹצָה.** וּכְגוֹן שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְדוּפָה עַד הֵנָּה לֵילֵךְ לְבֵית אָבִיהָ תָּדִיר, הִלְכָּךְ מְסַפְּקָא לָן בְּהֵי נִיחָא לַהּ:


תאכל במקום שהיא רוצה – and such is the case where she is not pursued until now to go regularly to the house of her father. Therefore, it is doubtful to us which is preferable to her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:1:4)


**אַפִּטְרוֹפְּסִים.** יָתוֹם שֶׁהָיוּ לוֹ שְׁנֵי אַפִּיטְרוֹפְּסִין וְהִמְנוּהוּ זֶה עַל פִּסְחוֹ וְזֶה עַל פִּסְחוֹ:


אפטרופין – an orphan that had two guardians/administrators and he assigned this one for his Passover offering and that one for his Passover offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:1:5)


**לֹא יֹאכַל מִשֶּׁל שְׁנֵיהֶם.** לֹא מִפִּסְחוֹ שֶׁל זֶה וְלֹא מִפִּסְחוֹ שֶׁל זֶה, דְּמִי נָתַן רְשׁוּת לַחֵלֶק הָאֶחָד לְהִמָּנוֹת עִם זֶה. וְאֵין לוֹ תַקָּנָה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן רָצוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם שֶׁיִּמָּנֶה עִם הָאֶחָד:


לא יאכל משל שניהם – neither from the Passover offering of this one, nor from the Passover offering of that one. For who gave permission to divide the one to be appointed with this one and he has no remedy other that if the both of them wanted to be appointed with the one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:1:6)


**לֹא יֹאכַל מִשֶּׁל רַבּוֹ.** אֲבָל מִשֶּׁל עַצְמוֹ אוֹכֵל, דְּכֵיוָן דְּדִינָא הָוֵי דְּכוֹפִין אֶת רַבּוֹ וְכוֹתֵב לוֹ גֵּט שִׁחְרוּר, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא נִשְׁתַּחְרֵר הֲרֵי הוּא כְבֶן חוֹרִין, הִלְכָּךְ אוֹכֵל מִשֶּׁל עַצְמוֹ:


לא יאכל משל רבו – but he eats from his own [only] and since it is the law that they force the master and he writes a bill of manumission for him, even though he has is not yet freed, he is like a free person. Therefore, he eats of his own.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:2:1)


**שָׁחַט גְּדִי יֹאכַל.** רַבּוֹ מִמֶּנּוּ. וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּבְכָל שְׁאָר פְּסָחִים רָגִיל תָּדִיר בְּטָלֶה, כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא פֵרֵשׁ לוֹ עֲלֵיהּ סָמֵיךְ:


שחט גדי יאכל – his master [should eat] from him [the kid] and even though all of the other Passover offerings are regular, the frequent is nullified since he didn’t express to him that he relied on it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:2:2)


**יֹאכַל מִן הָרִאשׁוֹן.** וְהַשֵּׁנִי יִשָּׂרֵף. וּבַגְּמָרָא מוֹקֵי לַהּ דַּוְקָא בְמֶלֶךְ וּמַלְכָּה, אִית דְּאָמְרֵי מִשּׁוּם שְׁלוֹם מַלְכוּת, וְאִית דְּאָמְרֵי מִפְּנֵי שֶׁסּוֹמְכִין עַל עַבְדֵיהֶם וְאֵין מַקְפִּידִים עַל סְעוּדָתָן אִם גְּדָיִים אִם טְלָאִים, הִלְכָּךְ יֹאכַל מִן הָרִאשׁוֹן כֵּיוָן דְּלֵיכָּא קְפִידָא, דְּבָרִאשׁוֹן יָצְאוּ יְדֵי חוֹבָתָן. אֲבָל אֱנָשֵׁי דְעָלְמָא דְּקָפְדֵי, לֹא יֹאכַל לֹא מִן הָרִאשׁוֹן וְלֹא מִן הַשֵּׁנִי, דְּקָיְמָא לָן אֵין נִמְנִין עַל שְׁנֵי פְסָחִים כְּאֶחָד לֶאֱכֹל אֵיזֶה מֵהֶם שֶׁיִּרְצֶה, דְּאֵין בְּרֵרָה, וְכִי בָעֵי לְמֵיכַל מֵהָא, דִּלְמָא בִּשְׁעַת שְׁחִיטָה לֹא הֲוָה דַעְתֵּיהּ עֲלֵיהּ:


יאכל מן הראשון – and the second one should be burned. And in the Gemara (Pesahim 88b) maintains that this is specifically with a king and a queen; there are those who say that it is because of the peace of the kingdom, and there are those who say that because they rely on their servants and they are not exacting about their [Passover] meal, whether with kids or lambs. Therefore, he should eat from the first, since there is no intimation that you care for a thing to be exactly as you want it, for with the first, they have fulfilled their religious obligations. But the people of the world that are particular, he should not either from the first nor the second, for we hold that they are not registered with two Passover offerings as one, to eat from whichever of them that he wants, for there is no retrospective designation. For when he wanted to eat from this one, perhaps at the time of slaughtering, mind was not upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:2:3)


**שָׁכַח מָה שֶּׁאָמַר.** שֶׁפֵּרֵשׁ לוֹ גְּדִי אוֹ טָלֶה, וְזֶה שָׁכַח:


שכח מה שאמר – that he expressed to him a kid or a lamb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:2:4)


**וְטָלֶה שֶׁלִּי.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקֵי לַהּ כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָלַךְ הָעֶבֶד אֵצֶל רוֹעֶה הָרָגִיל אֵצֶל רַבּוֹ וְרוֹצֶה בְתַקָּנָתוֹ, וְאָמַר לוֹ, אִם גְּדִי אָמַר לְךָ רַבְּךָ, גְּדִי שֶׁלּוֹ וְטָלֶה שֶׁלְּךָ עַל מְנָת שֶׁאֵין לְרַבְּךָ רְשׁוּת בּוֹ, דִּכְהַאי גַּוְנָא קָנָה הָעֶבֶד. דְּאִי לָאו הָכִי מַה שֶּׁקָּנָה עֶבֶד קָנָה רַבּוֹ, וְנִמְצְאוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם שֶׁל רַבּוֹ:


וטלה שלי – In the Gemara (Pesahim 88b) maintains it such as the case where the servant when to the shepherd where his master is accustomed and who wants his remedy (i.e., that things go right for the master), and he (i.e., the shepherd) said to him: “If it is a kid,” your master would tell you that the kid is his and that the lam is your on the condition that your master will not have possession of it,” and in such a manner, the servant acquires itr, for it had not been like this, what the servant acquired, the master acquired and it would be that both of them belong to the master.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:2:5)


**וּפְטוּרִים מִלַּעֲשׂוֹת פֶּסַח שֵׁנִי.** דְּאַף עַל גַּב דִּשְׁנֵיהֶם יוֹצְאִים לְבֵית הַשְּׂרֵפָה וְאֵינָן נֶאֱכָלִין מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵין הַמְּנוּיִין נִכָּרִים, מִיהוּ שְׁחִיטָה וּזְרִיקָה מְעַלְיָתָא הִיא, חֲדָא אַהַאי וַחֲדָא אַהַאי, דְּקַמֵּי שְׁמַיָּא גַּלְיָא:


ופטורים מלעשות פסח שני – and even though both of them go out to the place of burning and are not eaten because their registration was not known. However, the slaughtering and sprinkling [of the blood] are valid, one for this one and one for the other one, for before Heaven it is revealed.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:3:1)


**עַל מִי שֶׁיַּעֲלֶה רִאשׁוֹן לִירוּשָׁלַיִם.** כְּדֵי לְזָרְזָן קָאָמַר, שֶׁיִּזְדָּרֵז כָּל אֶחָד לִהְיוֹת רִאשׁוֹן. וּמִיהוּ כָּל בָּנָיו הִמְנָה עַל הַפֶּסַח, אֶלָּא שֶׁאוֹתוֹ שֶׁיַּעֲלֶה רִאשׁוֹן יִזְכֶּה רִאשׁוֹן וְכָל אֶחָיו יִזְכּוּ אַחֲרָיו עַל יָדוֹ:


ON HE WHO ARRIVES IN JERUSALEM FIRST - To encourage them, that they will all try to be first. All children will be on the paschal offering but he who comes first merits first and everyone else does so after him, due to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:3:2)


**עַד שֶׁיִּזְרֹק הַדָּם.** לְעוֹלָם יָכוֹל לִמָּשֵׁךְ, אֲפִלּוּ אַחַר שְׁחִיטָה, עַד שֶׁיִּזְרֹק הַדָּם. וּמוֹדֶה רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דְּאֵין נִמְנִין אֶלָּא עַד שֶׁיִּשְׁחַט. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


עד שיזרוק הדם – he can take possession, even after the slaughtering until the blood is tossed. But Rabbi Shimon admits that they are not registered other than until it is slaughtered. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:4:1)


**הַמַּמְנֶה אֲחֵרִים עַל חֶלְקוֹ.** אֶחָד מִבְּנֵי חֲבוּרָה שֶׁהִמְנָה אֲחֵרִים עַל חֶלְקוֹ, בְּלֹא דַעַת חֲבוּרָה:


הממנה אחרים על חלקו – one from the members of the group that registered others in his share, without the knowledge of the group.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:4:2)


**רַשָּׁאִין בְּנֵי חֲבוּרָה לוֹמַר לוֹ.** טֹל אֶת שֶׁלְּךָ וְצֵא וֶאֱכֹל אַתָּה וַחֲבֵרֶיךָ, שֶׁאֵין בְּנֵי חֲבוּרָה מִתְרַצִּים לִהְיוֹת כָּל אֵלּוּ הַדֵּעוֹת בַּחֲבוּרָתָן. וְאִית לֵיהּ לְהַאי תַּנָּא פֶּסַח נֶאֱכָל בִּשְׁתֵּי חֲבוּרוֹת:


ראשין בני חבורה לומר לו – “take what is yours, and go out and eat, you and your friends, for the members of the group are appeased/reconciled to have all these opinions in their group. And this Tanna has a Passover offering that is eaten by two groups (see Mishnah Pesahim, Chapter 7, Mishnah 13)



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:5:1)


**זָב שֶׁרָאָה שְׁתֵּי רְאִיּוֹת.** שֶׁטָּמֵא טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה וְאֵין טָעוּן קָרְבָּן:


זב שראה שתי ראיות – for he is defiled for a seven day period, and does not require a sacrifice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:5:2)


**שׁוֹחֲטִין עָלָיו.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא הֶעֱרִיב שִׁמְשׁוֹ. וְהוּא שֶׁטָּבַל בִּשְׁעַת הַשְּׁחִיטָה, דְּהָא חֲזִי לְמֵיכַל לְאוּרְתָא. דְּדַוְקָא טְמֵא מֵת שֶׁחָל שְׁבִיעִי שֶׁלּוֹ בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר נִדְחֶה לְפֶסַח שֵׁנִי אַף עַל גַּב דַּחֲזִי לְמֵיכַל לְאוּרְתָא, כְּדִכְתִיב (במדבר ט) וַיְהִי אֲנָשִׁים אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ טְמֵאִים לְנֶפֶשׁ אָדָם וְלֹא יָכְלוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת הַפֶּסַח בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא, בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא לֹא הָיוּ יְכוֹלִים, הָא לָעֶרֶב הָיוּ יְכוֹלִים לֶאֱכֹל בַּפֶּסַח, וְנִדְחוּ לְפֶסַח שֵׁנִי. אֲבָל שְׁאָר טֻמְאוֹת אִי חֲזִי לְמֵיכַל לְאוּרְתָא שׁוֹחֲטִים עָלָיו, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא הֶעֱרִיב שִׁמְשׁוֹ:


שוחטין עליו – even though the sun had not set and he who immersed at the time of the slaughter [of the Passover offering],is worthy to eat it in the evening, and especially someone who was impure who came in contact with the dead whose seventh day occurs on the fourteenth [of Nisan] is postponed until the Second Passover (14 Iyyar), even though it is proper to eat it at night, as it is written (Numbers 9:6): “But there were some men who were unclean by reason of a corpse and could not offer the Passover sacrifice on that day.” On that day they were not able but in the evening they were able to eat the Passover offering, but they are postponed until the Second Passover. But other defilements, if they are worthy to eat at night, we slaughter for him and even though sunset had not come.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:5:3)


**רָאָה שָׁלֹשׁ.** לָא חֲזִי לְמֵיכַל עַד דְּמַיְתֵי כַפָּרָה:


ראה שלש – he was not worthy to eat until he brought atonement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:5:4)


**שׁוֹחֲטִין עָלָיו.** אִם חָל שְׁמִינִי שֶׁלּוֹ בְּעֶרֶב פֶּסַח, אֲפִלּוּ לֹא הֵבִיא כַפָּרָתוֹ. וְהוּא שֶׁנָּתַן קָרְבְּנוֹתָיו לְבֵית דִּין:


שוחטין עליו – if his eighth day fell on the Eve of Passover, even if he did not bring his atonement. But he gave his sacrifices to the Jewish court.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:5:5)


**שׁוֹמֶרֶת יוֹם כְּנֶגֶד יוֹם.** הָרוֹאָה תּוֹךְ אַחַד עָשָׂר יוֹם שֶׁבֵּין נִדָּה לְנִדָּה דְּבָעְיָא לִסְפֹּר יוֹם הַמָּחֳרָת, שׁוֹחֲטִין עָלֶיהָ בְּיוֹם הַסְּפִירָה. דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁסָּפְרָה מִקְצָת הַיּוֹם מֻתֶּרֶת לִטְבֹּל. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמְּחֻסֶּרֶת הֶעֱרֵב שֶׁמֶשׁ שׁוֹחֲטִין עָלֶיהָ:


שומרת יום כנגד יום – Whomever sees [blood] within the eleven days between one menstruation and the [next] menstruation , that is required to court the day afterwards, we slaughter for her on the day of her counting. But since she counted part of the day, she is permitted to immerse [in the ritual bath]. And even though she is lacking sunset [which had not yet arrived], we slaughter [the Passover offering] on her behalf.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Pesachim 8:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Pesachim/r/8:5:6)


**וְהַזָּבָה.** שֶׁרָאֲתָה שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים רְצוּפִים בְּתוֹךְ אַחַד עָשָׂר יוֹם וּצְרִיכָה לִסְפֹּר שִׁבְעָה נְקִיִּים וּלְהָבִיא קָרְבָּן, שׁוֹחֲטִין עָלֶיהָ בַּשְּׁמִינִי:


והזבה – who saw [flux] on three consecutive days within the eleven [days between menstruation cycles], must count seven clean [days] and bring a sacrifice, we slaughter [a Passover offering] on her behalf on the eighth [day].
